A direct click/tap on the body of an eligible owner's car opens a responsive read-only card with an enlarged TrafficCars preview in a front-three-quarter view, showing value, Body/Wheels/Engine, oil/dirt, and selected owner-pipeline stages. The card does not dim the world and in desktop landscape reserves the bottom-right strip for the objective-card. For a closed issue, only the left half with the name is strikethrough, while on the right after a slash, the actual value increase is shown: core bonuses are taken from <code>DealerCarConfig.PARTS</code>, service gains are calculated from authoritative <code>baseValue + partsValue</code> and sequential completed multipliers. If an issue exists but the required stage is missing due to absent business, the line shows <code>NEED REPAIR BAY</code> or <code>NEED CAR WASH</code>; initially correct state shows <code>NO LEAK</code>/<code>CLEAN</code> without fake profit. World raycast works for full DealerCar and registered service/delivery proxies, while unaccepted seller cars are not clickable. The source of truth is owner-only <code>CarState v5</code>, including <code>workings</code>, <code>displays</code>, and <code>processings</code>; the UI does not introduce a new remote and survives world/service proxy recreation due to key <code>tradeId</code>.

The purchased staff Junk Spot, Used Cars, Repair Bay, Tow Truck Driver, and all five Tuning Atelier roles use a single <code>StaffInspectionService</code>/<code>StaffInspectionClient</code> contract. For each live rig, the server creates a transparent query-only <code>StaffInspectHitbox</code> of size <code>5 × 6.5 × 4</code>, welds it to <code>HumanoidRootPart</code> with a local Y-offset of 0.75, and forcibly sets <code>CanQuery=true</code>; the size is no longer computed through unstable <code>Model:GetBoundingBox()</code>. The client raycasts desktop mouse release or mobile <code>TouchTapInWorld</code> and sends only <code>StaffInspectRequest(staffId)</code>; there is no server/runtime ClickDetector. The server revalidates owner, purchased pad, exact rig, and distance ≤24 studs, after which the card displays a stable name, role, responsibilities, work zones, and authoritative metric, and the client locally highlights associated zones. For Tuning, specific cards for Dyno/Body/Paint Specialist, Service Advisor, and Operations Manager are defined. Staff and Car inspection use a shared client attribute <code>ActiveInspectionCard</code> and are strictly mutually exclusive. UI and processed input do not pass into world selection; closing the current card and selecting the next target are separate taps. Short click-through suppression is preserved for the explicit X cross.

On loading a previously saved profile with a persisted time anchor and a positive offline reward, the client displays a separate modal over a light dimming. On phone, the card has 520×208 px (in portrait, width is limited to viewport − 32): the sum and yellow label <code>EARNED WHILE YOU WERE AWAY · Nm</code> are on the same line, while <code>COLLECT</code> and inactive placeholder <code>2× · 49 R$</code> are on the same action-row with 56 px touch targets. The line <code>ALREADY ADDED TO YOUR BALANCE</code> is removed. The first modal instance is not created. The modal waits for release of another <code>MVPDealModal</code>, so it does not intercept seller/customer/inspection card interactions.

### Personal Garage UI, Phone and customize camera [LIVE IN OPEN STUDIO — 2026-08-30]

After tutorial, the left rail has <code>CAR INDEX</code> and one <code>PHONE</code>; the separate Car/Garage icon is no longer present. Phone opens <code>PersonalPhoneDesktopGui</code>, assembled on a clone of the standard <code>PlayerGui.Phone.Phone</code>: reused <code>Back</code>, texture <code>rbxassetid://89923132654814</code> and authored Screen inset; a separate black phone placeholder is not canonical. In desktop app <code>GARAGE</code> opens the active personal car/garage, while <code>BUY CARS</code> opens the full personal market. Before selecting a starter garage, it is hidden and Buy Cars occupies the first position; after selecting Garage/Buy Cars, they have LayoutOrder 1/2. The legacy fence Phone hides after a shared rail click to prevent two modals from overlapping.

Responsive contract separates phone, tablet, and desktop, and explicitly restores the geometry of the current profile on every viewport change. The actual PhoneLandscape Personal Garage on iPhone 13 safe viewport 749×368 has a panel of 494×274 px, cards viewport of 474×170 px and car row of 468×138 px: one car fits entirely within a single swipe, and the start of the next card hints at scrolling. <code>SEND TO GARAGE</code> and <code>SPAWN CAR</code> have touch target 120×56 and compact visual surface 116×38; Close has target 56×56 and visual size 34×34. Tablet and desktop maintain separate authored geometry; Phone desktop scales uniformly within the safe area, and returning-player offer uses its own modal contract. Primary mobile actions maintain touch targets of at least 56 px even when the visual surface is smaller.

Car previews use front three-quarter framing, calculated from model pivot/bounds; rear-facing Wronkler and repeated Phone→Garage switching are fixed. Garage and market UI do not duplicate each other. Locked cars display unlock copy without overriding server state.

Customize — right-side garage panel without fullscreen dimmer. When opening the original car, only the local view is hidden and replaced with a static preview; the server state does not change until Apply. Phone branch (<code>TouchEnabled</code> and viewport height ≤ 500) has width <code>clamp(X × 0.39, 276, 292)</code>, height <code>clamp(Y − 82, 252, 276)</code>, fixed compact header and vertical scroll; compact heights for Neon/Nitro/Body/Tint are equal to 166/112/166/264 px, swatches and actions are 44 px. iPhone 7 has 276×276 with scroll 264×206, Galaxy A06 — 276×256 with scroll 264×186; iPhone 17 uses 292×276. Tablet and desktop restore authored positions/sizes/fonts/canvas before their branch, so phone layout does not bleed between Device Simulator modes. Scriptable camera starts with a light auto-orbit, but the first mouse/touch drag transitions it to a permanent manual 360° orbit; mouse wheel and two-finger pinch adjust radius within range 17.5–23. The preview hit-area ends at the actual left boundary of the responsive panel. Close, Cancel, death, respawn, car switch/despawn and <code>CurrentCamera</code> replacement must fully restore camera, FOV, controls, AutoRotate, seat and prompts.

Customize sections are billed separately. Body Paint shows Factory and four Paint Atelier swatches, owned state and Buy/Select. Window Tint shows three level-buttons 0/50/100 and five color swatches black/red/yellow/cyan/magenta; level and color have separate Apply. Neon shows five approved colors, On/Off and Install/Apply; Nitro shows Install/Installed and keyboard/gamepad/touch hints. Locked copy directly names <code>TA_PaintCanisters</code>, <code>TA_BodyKitStation</code>, <code>TA_Neon</code> and <code>TA_NOS</code>. All swatches/level-buttons have 44 px primary dimension; text and buttons use normal outline/stroke contrasts, gradient is not directly overlaid on glyph-bearing TextButton.

## 10. Network contracts and server authority [LIVE]

### Runtime remotes

In Edit, 21 <code>TycoonRemotes</code> are visible. Runtime adds:

- <code>PadPurchased</code> — <code>{ padId, pad, target, title, effectText }</code> for departure pad, general build spark and local purchase-effect popup;
- <code>StaffInspectRequest</code>;
- <code>StaffInspectionState</code>;
- <code>MVPDeviceClass</code>;
- <code>CarIndexState</code>;
- <code>RequestCarIndex</code>;
- <code>SellerArrivalNotice</code>;
- <code>EconomyRateUpdate</code>.

The exact number of children <code>TycoonRemotes</code> is not a contract: some channels are created at runtime. AI reading only Explorer in Edit sees an incomplete contract. <code>NPCService</code> additionally creates at runtime <code>ServiceVisualArrived</code> to confirm physical arrival via service proxy. Root remotes such as <code>RexVoiceRemote</code> and <code>AdminCommandFeedback</code> are considered separately.

### Client → server

| Remote | Client intent | Primary server validation |
|---|---|---|
| <code>BuyAction</code> | buy/haggle/refuse | action whitelist, tradeId, W index, exact active deal |
| <code>OfferAction</code> | accept/askmore/refuse | whitelist, exact active trade/state |
| <code>BuyPad</code> | buy next pad | server selects recommended pad, checks cash and dependencies |
| <code>StationClick</code> | manual station action | owner, real host, station type |
| <code>StationUpgrade</code> | upgrade Parts Sales Laptop | owner, unlock, max level, server price; sender exists only for Junk Spot Upgrade |
| <code>RequestCarState</code> | request snapshot | owner record, cooldown |
| <code>RequestProgressionState</code> | request objective DTO | server publish |
| <code>RequestCarIndex</code> | request catalog DTO | owner profile, no money |
| <code>ListingDriveInput</code> | legacy dealer-driving input | live sender/player-entry path removed; server compatibility validation does not create an active session itself |
| <code>ParkAssist</code> | legacy tutorial parking assist | live sender does not exist; current Rusty W→P parks FirstSalesman |
| <code>StaffInspectRequest</code> | inspect role | paid pad, exact rig, distance, ownership |
| <code>MVPDeviceClass</code> | platform class | desktop/mobile/console whitelist |
| <code>ServiceVisualArrived</code> | exact service proxy reaches final inbound marker | owner tycoon, exact slot/job/trade ID, active kind and state <code>Approaching</code> |
| <code>PersonalGarageRemote</code> | GetState / ChooseStarter / Buy / Activate / PrepareSell / ConfirmSell / Return / Summon / ApplyBodyColor / ApplyTintLevel / ApplyTintColor / ApplyNeon / InstallNitro / CloseCustomize | owner tycoon, tutorial, one-free-starter state, catalog/gate, active owned car, occupancy/customize state, safe summon ground, garage parking, terminal distance, per-section pad unlock, whitelisted palette/level, Cash, cooldown, 15-second one-use sell token and critical commit |
| <code>ListCar</code> | legacy refresh | does not commit listing |
| <code>RepairPart</code> | legacy | no server handler and no client sender |

Typical cooldowns: trade actions 0.2 seconds, BuyPad 0.5, ListCar 0.5, state request 1.0, progression request 0.5. Trade ID must be a string of limited length; slot/index and ownership are re-verified.

Key signatures:

- <code>BuyAction(action, tradeId?, workingIndex?)</code>;
- <code>OfferAction(action, tradeId?)</code>;
- <code>ListingDriveInput(throttle, steer)</code>;
- <code>StaffInspectRequest(staffId)</code>.

<code>StarterGui.CustomerUI.ToggleCard</code> — local <code>BindableEvent</code>, not network remote.

### Server → client

Main payload channels:

- <code>BuyUpdate</code> — world/fence offer or hide;
- <code>OfferUpdate</code> — buyer offer/breakdown or hide;
- <code>CarState</code> version 5 — W1/W2, P1…P6, processing capacity/stock, inspection data and compatibility aliases;
- <code>ProgressionState</code> — chapter, objectiveStep, tutorialComplete, revision, objective DTO;
- <code>CarIndexState</code> — unlocked ids, catalog rows, toast ids;
- <code>QueueUpdate</code>;
- <code>RepairVFX</code>;
- <code>CashPopup</code>, <code>InsufficientFunds</code>, <code>ProfitCard</code>;
- <code>ListingNotice</code>;
- <code>SellerArrivalNotice</code> — one <code>{ eventId, tradeId, workingIndex, carName }</code> after physical arrival and transition <code>Arriving=false</code>;
- <code>EconomyRateUpdate</code> — <code>{ windowSeconds=60, total }</code> for the actual business-revenue on the second row of CashPanel;
- <code>PadPurchased</code>;
- <code>StaffInspectionState</code>;
- <code>PersonalGarageState</code> — owner-only catalog, owned/active cars, unlocks, prices, per-car customization/ownership, <code>bodyPalette</code>, <code>tintLevels</code>, <code>tintPalette</code>, and base/salvage breakdown;
- <code>PersonalGarageCustomize</code> — validated physical terminal entry with active car and garage references for local preview/camera session.

Other runtime remotes:

- <code>SellCarsRadio.Control</code> — TogglePower/ChangeStation; only for real driver;
- <code>RexVoiceRemote</code> — server → client line ID;
- <code>AdminCommandFeedback</code> — server → authorized admin result for <code>/addmoney</code>/<code>/releasecar</code>; created in <code>ReplicatedStorage</code>, not inside <code>TycoonRemotes</code>;
- <code>DebugRemotes.GetState</code> and <code>DebugRemotes.Action</code> — RemoteFunctions replicated to all clients; owner/admin authorization is performed server-side, and object visibility is not a security boundary;
- <code>&lt;car&gt;.Remotes.SetNitroEnabled(enabled:boolean)</code>;
- <code>&lt;car&gt;.Remotes.SetLightState(braking:boolean, reversing:boolean, signal:string)</code>; both per-car handlers accept only the actual occupant canonical DriverSeat.

### Immutable security invariants

1. The client never sends the final payout, pad price, or new Cash balance.
2. The server recalculates value, offer, affordability, and progression.
3. Sale is idempotent through <code>resolvedTrades[tradeId]</code>.
4. A regular listing checks exact model, W, P, tradeId, state, owner, and achievement of a physical goal; an owner-pipeline listing checks exact persisted processing record/tradeId, free P, and critical transaction boundary.
5. The instance sent by the client must belong to the canonical tycoon/station/car.
6. Remote action whitelist and rate limit remain server-side.
7. Any new payout goes through one server-authoritative transaction boundary.

If a new feature requires trusting a number from a LocalScript, the feature architecture is incorrect.

## 11. Internal gameplay events and analytics [LIVE]

<code>GameplayEvents</code> creates a runtime folder <code>CoreGameplayEvents</code> and exactly 28 BindableEvents:

1. CarArrived
2. TradeOpened
3. CarBought
4. PartFixed
5. RepairCompleted
6. CarListed
7. OfferRequested
8. OfferReceived
9. TradeResolved
10. CarSold
11. PadPurchased
12. MechanicHired
13. SalesmanHired
14. RepairBayOpened
15. ServiceCompleted
16. PersonalCarStarterChosen
17. PersonalCarCustomized
18. PersonalCarSold
19. PersonalCarReplaced
20. PersonalCarEntered
21. PersonalCarDriven60Seconds
22. PersonalGarageExpanded
23. TrackRunStarted
24. TrackLapCompleted
25. TrackRunCompleted
26. CarPipelineTransition
27. PipelineQueueAge
28. SessionStarted

Contract: <code>event:Fire(player, shallowClonedPayload)</code>. Bus adds the current <code>objectiveStep</code>, if the producer does not explicitly pass it. The active <code>GameAnalytics</code> routes events to a specialized onboarding funnel and <code>AnalyticsService:LogCustomEvent</code>; the tutorial index has priority only for an exact deal 1, so a regular index 0 does not mask channel/state. Device class comes separately through <code>MVPDeviceClass</code>.

Tutorial measurement uses existing events without a second analytics stack: <code>PartFixed</code> carries <code>repairSource/manual/manualRepairCurrent/manualRepairTotal/valueGain</code>; <code>CarBought</code> — <code>action/askForLowerUsed</code>; <code>TradeResolved</code> is sent upon buyer actions; <code>OfferRequested</code> and <code>TradeResolved</code> are included in the map. Therefore, Cash Register, seller arrival, purchase, Engine/Wheels latency, FirstSalesman hire, listing, buyer offer, first sale, and completion are measurable. The Dealer-stock seat event is not needed, but a separate Personal Garage driving path is measured via server-side events <code>PersonalCarEntered</code> and <code>PersonalCarDriven60Seconds</code>.

Funnel <code>PersonalCarAdoption_v1</code> has three sequential persisted steps: <code>FirstPersonalCarEntered → PersonalCarDriven60Seconds → NeonPurchasedAndInstalled</code>. The second step counts only the time from owner occupant to actual chassis speed of at least 2 studs/s; idle in the seat is not counted. Neon step is placed only after a successfully paid first <code>neon_install</code>, not during recolor/toggle. Timestamps and <code>personalCarFunnelStep</code> are saved in profile, so rejoin, reboarding, and color change do not duplicate the funnel. <code>PersonalCarCustomized</code> and <code>PersonalCarSold</code> continue separately carrying operation/cost and base/salvage analytics.

The current end-to-end onboarding measures a separate persisted funnel <code>NewPlayerOnboarding_v2</code>. It continues the first business flip after <code>TutorialCompleted</code> steps <code>StarterPersonalCarChosen → FirstPersonalCarEntered → PersonalCarDriven60Seconds</code>. Cohort is fixed once via field <code>onboardingV2Eligible</code>: new players or those not completing tutorial enter, while legacy players who have already completed are excluded. Progress is stored in <code>onboardingV2Step</code>. The seating step and custom event <code>personal_car_entered_first</code> are server-authoritative and first-lifetime; they can be used as a direct indicator of the number of players who have actually taken the driver seat of their personal car.

Lot-service uses a whitelist event <code>ServiceCompleted</code>; the previous latent <code>LotServiceCompleted</code> has been removed. Dealer-stock service is activated via persisted <code>OwnerCarPipeline</code>; legacy <code>LOT_JOB_CHANCE=0</code> no longer manages dispatch and is retained only for rolling compatibility.

## 12. Saving and transactions [LIVE]

### DataStore

- Store: <code>SellCarsTycoon_v9_20260810_working2_stock6</code>.
- Key: <code>player_&lt;UserId&gt;</code>.
- Initial Cash: $3,300.
- Autosave: every 30 seconds, only dirty profile.
- Minimum interval for regular writes: 7 seconds.
- Load: up to 4 attempts with delays 2, 4, 6 seconds.
- Routine and critical writes use <code>UpdateAsync</code>.

### Save schema

~~~text
SaveData
├─ cash
├─ lastSeenAt                         # server timestamp for capped offline reward
├─ pads { padId → true }
├─ stationLevels { stationId → level }
├─ activeCar / activeCar2              # W1/W2; each ActiveCar contains pipeline + condition
├─ processingCars[]                    # up to 4 owner cars outside W: Pipeline/AwaitingDisplay
├─ incomingQueue[]
├─ displayCars { index → ActiveCar }   # P1…P6
├─ displayCar / displayCar2            # legacy P1/P2 aliases
├─ analytics
│  ├─ firstRepairAt / firstSaleAt / firstServiceAt
│  ├─ personalCarEnteredAt / personalCarDriven60At / personalCarNeonPurchasedAt
│  ├─ personalCarFunnelStep
│  └─ repairCount / saleCount
├─ personalGarage
│  ├─ starterChosen
│  ├─ starterRecoveryVersion
│  ├─ freeStarterCarId?
│  ├─ owned { carId → true }
│  ├─ activeCarId
│  ├─ expanded / secondaryCarId?
│  ├─ trackCooldownUntil / trackIntroCompleted
│  └─ customizations { carId → PersonalCarCustomizationSave }
│     ├─ neonInstalled / neonColorId / neonEnabled / nitroInstalled
│     ├─ bodyColorId? / ownedBodyColorIds { colorId → true }
│     ├─ tintLevelId / ownedTintLevelIds { "0"|"50"|"100" → true }
│     └─ tintColorId / ownedTintColorIds { black|red|yellow|cyan|magenta → true }
├─ tutorial
│  ├─ eligible / dealIndex / completed
│  └─ rushAvailable / rushCompleted
├─ progression
│  ├─ chapter / objectiveStep
│  ├─ tutorialComplete
│  └─ revision
├─ resolvedTrades { tradeId → true }
├─ carIndex
└─ served { personId → count }
~~~

Element <code>incomingQueue[]</code> stores <code>queueId/templateId/ask/sellerId</code> and an optional backward-compatible <code>source="tow"</code>. Tow source does not change price or deal rules; it is needed for restoration and origin marking in queue presentation. Delivery explicitly saves the profile after atomic handoff, while owner release or reset invalidates the generation token, releases reservation, and does not create a duplicate.

<code>lastSeenAt</code> is updated in snapshot/leave path. On next load, the server considers absence, ignores intervals shorter than 90 seconds, limits calculation to eight hours, adds base reward to Cash before leaderstats creation, and confirms the change via <code>commitCritical("offline_earnings")</code>. A profile without the field receives the current timestamp and does not receive retroactive windfall.

Exact set <code>ActiveCarSave</code>:

- <code>templateId</code>, <code>state</code>;
- <code>parts = {Body, Wheels, Engine}</code>;
- <code>haggleUsed</code>, <code>offered</code>, <code>ask</code>, <code>cost</code>;
- <code>serviced</code>, <code>atBay</code>, <code>serviceRollDone</code>, <code>serviceQueuedAt</code>;
- <code>sellerId</code>, <code>customerId</code>, <code>tradeId</code>;
- <code>tutorialDealIndex</code>, <code>tutorialWildcard</code>;
- frozen <code>listedPrice</code>;
- <code>pipeline = {revision, state, plan[], nextIndex, completed{}, currentStage, currentSlot, queuedAt, escortId}</code>;
- <code>condition = {dirty, oilLeak}</code>;
- <code>factoryPaintId</code>, <code>paintHue</code>, <code>neonColorId</code>;
- <code>lastServiceStage</code>, <code>lastServiceSlot</code>;
- for writes inside <code>processingCars</code>: <code>processingStatus = Pipeline | AwaitingDisplay</code> and original <code>sourceWorkingIndex</code>.

Runtime compatibility aliases <code>washed/tuned*</code> may be set by service code, but the persisted source of truth is <code>pipeline.completed</code>; sanitizer does not save them separately.

One-Rusty tutorial does not add persisted fields: authored start is derived from <code>tutorialDealIndex=1</code>, and partial Engine/Wheels progress remains in existing <code>parts</code>. Each successful <code>repairPart</code> marks the profile as dirty. Rolling sanitize sets previous profiles with <code>dealIndex&gt;1</code> <code>completed=true, dealIndex=2</code>; remaining old deals 2/3 in W, processing, or P retain exact identity but become ordinary stock with <code>tutorialDealIndex=0</code> and without wildcard tutorial rules.

### Load failure

After a full load failure, the player receives a blank session-only profile with <code>loaded=false</code>; saving is disabled. Critical pad purchase fails closed, but not all regular economy paths explicitly check this mode. Consider such a session as failed and do not promise the player data persistence.

### Sanitize and migrations

Supported:

- legacy <code>stationLevel</code> → <code>stationLevels.junk</code>;
- <code>DisplayPad</code> → <code>RepairPad</code>;
- old Repair Bay/Car Wash bundles → new pad sets;
- P1/P2 aliases ↔ generalized <code>displayCars</code>;
- legacy <code>serviced/atBay/serviceRollDone</code> → canonical lowercase owner pipeline; old owner records with <code>queued/servicing/AwaitingDisplay</code> are moved from W to <code>processingCars</code>, stale servicing lease without live slot is returned to queued, and legacy displayed stock migrates to complete/clean;
- unknown pipeline stages/states are dropped, stage order is canonicalized;
- dropping unknown pad/state and clamping numbers;
- profile without tutorial schema → tutorial complete;
- incomplete old tutorial deal 2/3 → ordinary stock with exact car identity/state and <code>tutorialDealIndex=0</code>; tutorial is normalized to complete/dealIndex 2;
- chapter is not reduced;
- objective is recalculated in canonical order.
- legacy profiles without <code>personalGarage</code> receive a blank collection; known catalog IDs are preserved, unknown ones are discarded; customization safely migrates to factory/off state;
- one-time migration <code>starterRecoveryVersion=1</code> restores Rusty as active/free starter only to the old profile with already used starter choice and empty collection; the version is set even if recovery is not needed, so free distribution never repeats;
- veterans with previously used <code>trackCooldownUntil</code> sanitizer are set to <code>trackIntroCompleted=true</code>; others retain false until the first completion of MainTrack;
- legacy personal-car customization without new cosmetics receives <code>bodyColorId=nil</code>, empty <code>ownedBodyColorIds</code>, selected+owned tint level <code>"0"</code> and selected+owned tint color <code>black</code>. Sanitizer preserves only whitelisted owned IDs and accepts selected body/tint only if the corresponding owned license is present; otherwise, it returns a safe default;
- personal-car analytics timestamps and funnel step from missing old profiles are added as nil/0 without retroactive events.

### Critical transactions

<code>tryPurchasePad</code> atomically changes Cash, leader pad, bundle members, and progression. On write failure, memory mutation is rolled back.

<code>trySettleTrade</code> atomically adds payout and <code>resolvedTrades[tradeId]</code>; repeated payout is rejected.

Personal Garage purchase/sale/customization first modifies authoritative Cash + <code>personalGarage</code> snapshot, then calls <code>commitCritical("personal_garage_…")</code>. Sale mutation additionally requires a fresh one-use token from <code>PrepareSell</code>; <code>ConfirmSell</code> rechecks ownership, sole-starter guard, and token TTL. On failure, Cash, owned/active IDs, and all per-car customization, including nested ownership maps, are rolled back; visual Body Paint/Window Tint/Neon/Nitro and respawn are applied only after commit. Cosmetic color/level payments do not alter salvage basis.

Tow Truck first atomically transfers exact cargo to the authoritative incoming FIFO and only then completes the job-driven station job. Key <code>tow:&lt;queueId&gt;</code> prevents one delivery from paying again during local retry; money is not issued until successful <code>enqueueTowDelivery</code>.

<code>commitCritical</code> is serialized with a lock, has a deadline of about 20 seconds, three attempts, and backoff. Leave and shutdown trigger a flush; shutdown deadline is bounded.

<code>DataService.save()</code> means that routine save is accepted into the queue/coalescing, not that data is already durable in DataStore. Any monetary or irreversible operation after which a world object is freed must use a confirmed <code>commitCritical</code>. <code>BindToClose</code> has a general budget of about 5 seconds in Studio and 3 seconds in production.

> DataStore renaming is a new season/reset, not a regular refactor. Never change store name without a migration plan, rollback, and a decision on what to do with old profiles.

### Known persistence risks

1. **No cross-server session lock.** UpdateAsync returns a full local snapshot and does not use a lease/session token. Two servers with the same UserId can perform a last-writer-wins overwrite.
2. **Some durable mutations do not mark dirty immediately.** Station earnings/upgrades and individual repair changes usually end up in leave flush or subsequent save, but a crash before that creates a window of loss.
3. Studio playtest uses the real DataStore if the API is available. A test on the main account can alter a production-like profile.
4. Save code contains local locks, but this does not replace cross-server locking.

---

## 13. Assets and models [LIVE]

**Suspension acceptance and texture loading — V3.59:** standard [vehicle integration](SELL_CARS_VEHICLE_INTEGRATION_RU.md), §4, includes a verified case with Gblock: suspension body oscillation and wheel shaking caused an unstable combination of suspension/anti-roll settings; disabling body collision did not help. For its specific rig, suspension damping `350/350`, anti-roll stiffness `16000/16000`, anti-roll damping `100/100` were adopted with standard collisions enabled and a stabilizer. This is not a general SUV preset. Separate occupied idle and driving checks are required, followed by a retry after new Play; neutral axes alone do not prove stability. Assigned SurfaceAppearance asset IDs do not guarantee texture loading: check fetch status, Studio errors, and rendering. In previous interior maps of Gblock, `Failure / HttpError: DnsResolve` was observed. After assigning custom maps from the inventory group District Bloom, a new Play showed seat and instrument panel texture, although preloaded string IDs continued to return Failure. Therefore, preloading is a supportive diagnostic: acceptance requires checking ownership and assigning maps, verifying actual errors, and real rendering. Importing into Asset Manager does not automatically assign maps to an existing MeshPart; search for assets by the user-specified creator, including groups. The policy for ready Substance Painter exports and existing VehiclePaint from V3.58 is preserved.

### Vehicles

#### Mandatory integration standard for vehicles [CONTRACT — 2026-09-10]

Full instruction: [SELL_CARS_VEHICLE_INTEGRATION_RU.md](SELL_CARS_VEHICLE_INTEGRATION_RU.md). Asset preparation in Studio: [SELL_CARS_VEHICLE_EDITING_GUIDE_RU.md](SELL_CARS_VEHICLE_EDITING_GUIDE_RU.md). Source audit: [VEHICLE_INTEGRATION_AUDIT_2026-09-10.md](artifacts/VEHICLE_INTEGRATION_AUDIT_2026-09-10.md).

When requesting "integrate a vehicle", the AI applies the full standard without re-listing it to the owner. Before a new integration, it reads existing data and presents only the remaining questions as a single list: new vehicle or replacement/ID; body type and purpose; economic class/tier; when purchase and arrival open; personal price separately from dealer cost/value/rarity; speed/acceleration/handling; participation in purchases, races, Car Index, repair arrivals, and street traffic; presence of FBX/deformation currently or explicitly deferred morph; hidden meshes/FX and cracked glass; default color and palette. When replacing an existing vehicle, previously agreed-upon parameters are preserved without re-verification. Independent diagnostics and staged preparation are allowed before responses, but silent balance assignment is prohibited.

Body type (SUV/jeep, pickup, sedan, hatchback, coupe) and performance segment (sports car/hypercar) are not equal <code>DealerCarConfig.CarClass=low/mid/high/hyper</code>. The AI independently selects the closest working donor based on geometry, wheelbase/track, seating, mass, and handling; this is a technical obligation, not a decision the owner must make. Wheel axes, tie rods, toe/camber, and wheel positioning under the driver are verified; wheels must not be oriented inward, and the vehicle design must not be adjusted to fit an awkward rig.

Minimum execution contract:

1. Cross-check the Bible, new import, and current Studio source via MCP; select the exact Studio instance and save recovery. The new visual is placed on a proven working rig without losing mechanics, seat, or runtime identity.
2. For a new ID, register <code>DealerCarConfig</code>/Car Index, personal purchase price/catalog, UI stats, and real physics targets, <code>RacingConfig.CARS + CAR_ORDER</code>, dealer/repair selector, traffic, and per-car profile/sanitizer consumers. By default, a new vehicle must be available for purchase, selectable in races, and arrive for repairs; exceptions are recorded in intake. Only the model in storage does not count as integration.
3. In the current source <code>PersonalGarageService</code> creates a personal CATALOG only if <code>PERSONAL_PRICES[template.id]</code> exists; Racing has a separate whitelist and is not automatically populated from BUY CARS. <code>topSpeedKph/handlingRating/accelerationRating</code> — catalog readout: separately configure and verify physics. The current race-spec model does not imply mandatory personal ownership; a separate race gate requires explicit approval.
4. Transfer and adjust interior, plates, and missing lighting. Rear turn signals L/R, brakes, reverse, and front lenses must be visible from outside; configuration of <code>Lights</code> and <code>VehicleProfiles.lightFollowers</code> is mandatory. Turn signals must not be automatically mirrored to the opposite side.
5. Transfer transforms relative to the vehicle's root pivot; after modifying lighting/plates/DriverSeat, re-weld and verify after unanchoring. All required wheel↔body/chassis <code>NoCollisionConstraint</code> have live endpoints. <code>VehicleSeat</code>: Transparency 1, CastShadow false.
6. Cabin glass according to the working baseline — Glass/Transparency 0.75, preserving original Color/Reflectance. Broken glass — a separate Rusty-style overlay; clean personal/preview does not contain cracks. Full tint — optical black RGB(2,2,3), Transparency 0, SmoothPlastic, Reflectance 0.025; 0% restores the authored state. The old colored 100% tint is superseded.
7. Use pre-existing authored textures from Substance Painter: the current folder `Textures` and verified connections in Studio. Preserve Color/Normal/Roughness/Metallic, UV, and body, wheel, and interior details. During integration, **do not generate, redraw, or desaturate textures, do not create separate color variant images**; image changes are only permitted upon explicit user request. Neutral Base Color verification does not allow re-creation. Colors are defined via existing shared `VehiclePaint` and `SurfaceAppearance.Color`, only on explicit paint targets; interiors, wheels, optics, and glass are excluded. Maintain default + 2 factory colors. Do not return a canceled glossy repaint override and do not allow paint/tint/neon reset during service or sale handoff.
8. The presence of morph and hide list is determined by intake. If a morph exists, match the real FBX shape with the runtime mesh, verify Broken → repaired and FX. If not yet available, explicitly record deferred/none and integrate the remaining agreed repair capabilities without false MorphReady.
9. Synchronize all real full/source/traffic/reference consumers, preserving owner-authored and DoNotAutoSync exceptions. Verify purchase/gates, race, repair arrival, full occupied drive, lights/windows/condition/cosmetics, and handoffs in MCP Play. For UI/input order: iPhone → tablet → desktop. Return Studio to Edit; report actual verification and limitations, do not issue Edit inspection as Play acceptance.

**Open discrepancy in read-only audit 2026-09-10:** in <code>ServerStorage.CarModels.ChevroluxC10.Wheels.WheelFR/FL/RR/RL.Wheel.BodyNoCollisionConstraint</code> all four <code>Part0=nil</code>, <code>Part1</code> point to the wheel. The historically successful fix remains historical; today's template has not been proven as a valid donor. Runtime spawn and its influence on suspension in this audit were not tested, and the fix was not applied. Verified Bavora/FierroRossa/Hondo/Rusty did not have nil-endpoint NoCollisionConstraint; this is not full current acceptance of these vehicles.

#### Current repositories and punitive contracts

- <code>ReplicatedStorage.Car</code> — general drivable kiosk template.
- <code>ServerStorage.CarModels</code> — 13 full dealer/debug models.
- <code>ReplicatedStorage.TrafficCars</code> — lightweight traffic/service representations.
- <code>ReplicatedStorage.Templates.NPCS</code> — authored NPC rigs/cast.
- <code>ReplicatedStorage.CarConditionVisuals.DirtSurfaceAppearance</code> — preauthored transparent grime mask <code>rbxassetid://124804413176803</code>; runtime only clones it, because the record <code>SurfaceAppearance.ColorMap</code> from Script is prohibited by Roblox's capability model.
- <code>TuningAtelierTemplate.Building.AtelierProps.NOSStation</code> — canonical import from <code>Sell Cars - 3D Models</code>: MeshPart <code>rbxassetid://102421057309461</code> and ColorMap <code>rbxassetid://77647839294467</code>; the same model clone is used by the template and all eight plots.

Exact model IDs in <code>ServerStorage.CarModels</code>:

- eligible dealer pool: <code>RustyCar</code>, <code>Wronkler</code>, <code>ChevroluxC10</code>, <code>RadoBoxlet</code>, <code>NisshoSlideSX</code>, <code>HondoCivixEK</code>, <code>Bavora</code>, late <code>FierroRossa</code> / <code>Porschka911</code>, mid <code>Mercurion190r</code> and full-service hyper <code>ZentauriR</code> after respective gates;
- street/customer uses the same 11-car catalog; the current tutorial includes only Rusty and does not allow early Zentauri without full-service unlock;
- storage-only relative to current dealer config: <code>NormalCar</code>, <code>TowTruck</code>.

In Edit there exists a visual authored-prototype neon underglow for the second Body window in Tuning Atelier: <code>Workspace.EditorOnlyQueueSlots.TuningAtelierNeonReference</code>. On the free northern plot, in a single row, stand 11 catalog models (<code>RustyCar</code> → <code>ZentauriR</code>); service <code>NormalCar</code>/<code>TowTruck</code> are intentionally excluded. Each wrapper has <code>Vehicle</code> and an editable <code>NeonUnderglow</code> from four thin Neon cylinders <code>Neon_Left</code>, <code>Neon_Right</code>, <code>Neon_Front</code>, <code>Neon_Rear</code>, each with a child <code>PointLight Underglow</code>. After manual tuning by the owner, the current transforms of all 44 tubes are independent authored prototypes: <code>LayoutMode=PerCarUserAuthored</code>, each wrapper has <code>LayoutSource=UserAdjustedPerCar</code>/<code>LayoutStatus=ReferenceAccepted</code>, the previous derivation from RustyCar is removed. In <code>NeonColorPalette</code> v2 five variants are approved: Cyan <code>#00F5FF</code>, Bright Red <code>#FF3038</code>, Magenta <code>#FF2FD1</code>, Lime <code>#7CFF4A</code>, Orange <code>#FF7A21</code>. Blue and Violet are excluded as visually weak; Bright Red replaces Blue and uses an enhanced light <code>Brightness=1.70</code>/<code>Range=8</code> against the general <code>1.35/7</code>. Before vehicles <code>NeonPaletteReviewLine</code> displays five separate long Neon samples in the approved order, each with a matching PointLight and a label with name/HEX; status <code>OwnerApproved</code>. The production module <code>ReplicatedStorage.VehicleNeon</code> contains exact pivot-local CFrame/Size of all 44 tubes and this palette, creates massless/non-colliding welded cylinders for full dealer models and TrafficCars proxy and is able to interpolate positioning sequentially. The Edit folder remains visually editable source of truth; <code>HideEditorOnlyQueueSlots</code> fully removes it during Play, while runtime uses a compact module contract and does not depend on editor-only objects.

The local candidate set for wall posters in Tuning Atelier is stored in <code>assets/tuning_atelier/posters</code>: six full-bleed PNG formats ≈9:16 (<code>941×1672</code>) in the style of the current Roblox frame Sell Cars — simple low-poly vehicles, blocky NPCs, Plastic/SmoothPlastic and the familiar desert map. Three variants contain short captions <code>NO GLOW / NO GO</code>, <code>LOW CARS / HIGH STANDARDS</code>, <code>SELL IT? / SEND IT.</code>; another three work without text through visual jokes with incorrectly placed neon, Dyno smoke in the form of a question, and an overly large wing. These are local source assets/candidates: they have not yet been loaded into Roblox and are not placed in production Atelier.

Full-rig chassis runtime is centralized in <code>ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Vehicle.RuntimePackage</code>/<code>VehicleProfiles</code>; model-local vehicle scripts are removed. Changes to shared runtime still require verification of all 13 full profiles and lightweight <code>TrafficCars</code>, because geometry, optional components, and profile metadata remain hand-tuned.

Condition overlay does not replace the original SurfaceAppearance of the vehicle: a shared module creates non-collidable welded shells over one dominant body MeshPart and four wheel/tire MeshParts. The current validator confirms 5 targets across all 13 full and all 13 TrafficCars templates.

Dense dirt mask is loaded as <code>rbxassetid://82268692904522</code>; body/wheel tint is enhanced, but the overlay remains non-collidable. The canonical local source of the mask is <code>assets/vehicle_dirt_mask.png</code>.

The wheels of TrafficCars, dealer cars, and service proxy use a common contract: the spin axis is taken from a valid <code>SpinAxis</code>, and if it is missing, it is determined by the thinnest local mesh dimension. The rotation sign is normalized relative to +X in car space, so mirror-reversed right wheels such as HondoCivixEK, Mercurion190r, and similar imports physically rotate in the same direction as left wheels. All currently visible tyres of <code>ServerStorage.CarModels</code> have a clear <code>SpinAxis</code>.

<code>ChevroluxC10</code> retains the previously proven authored height of Chassis/DriverSeat and disables unstable front/rear anti-roll springs, but with change set 2.81 it uses a new visual FBX. Separate <code>L_Headlight</code> and <code>R_Headlight</code> provide visible neon geometry, while the preserved <code>Body.FrontLights</code> remains transparent to host two headlight attachments/SpotLights with a common dealer preset <code>Brightness=1.6</code>, <code>Range=28</code>, <code>Angle=52</code>, <code>Shadows=false</code>. Full-rig Play acceptance must verify the body-to-wheel gap, movement, steering, rotation of all four motor wheels, both front headlights, and Broken morph; the previous settled gap of approximately <code>0.94</code> stud is a historical benchmark of the old visual body and is not considered proven for the new geometry until runtime QA.

Each physical wheel <code>ChevroluxC10</code> has its own separate <code>BodyNoCollisionConstraint</code> to <code>Body.Collision.Chassis</code>. These four constraints are mandatory: after body replacement, old constraints could persist with <code>Part0=nil</code>, causing the collidable body chassis to squeeze the wheels, especially the rear left one.

For chassis fix:

1. identify all canonical copies;
2. fix the source of choice;
3. synchronously propagate the fix to all models;
4. verify kiosk car, dealer car, debug spawn, and traffic/service representation separately;
5. do not pull runtime dependency from dated archive.

### Template and archive folders

<code>RepairBay_New_Template</code>, <code>CarWash_UPD_AuthoredSource</code>, <code>CarWash_UPD_RuntimeTemplate</code>, <code>TuningAtelierTemplate</code> — authored/runtime production sources that must be distinguished by actual consumer. The tuning template contains canonical NOSStation and five staff rigs/markers; their local placement is transferred to the plot, not computed via roof-prone world raycast. <code>RB_Ceiling</code> uses front-center <code>CeilingPadBase</code>; base, <code>CeilingBuyPad</code> and labels move as a unified subtree and are synchronized on Tycoon1…Tycoon8 and <code>RepairBay_New_Template</code>. 

In <code>Buildings.RepairBay.Building.CheckoutExtension.CO_Walls2Model</code> there are two identical folders <code>Window</code>; they must be distinguished by the orientation of the glass relative to the walls, not by name or child order. The window chosen by the owner of Tycoon1 with <code>RightExteriorWindowGlass</code> near <code>(394.932, 8.160, 777.083)</code> is the authored-prototype of the corresponding facade window: its six components, including individually shortened and recentered upper and lower trim pieces, are rigidly transformed relative to <code>RightWall_FrontSegment</code> on Tycoon2…Tycoon8. The second identical window remained unchanged. <code>RepairBay_New_Template</code> received the same component-wise position/size deltas within its own geometry, not a blind world CFrame replacement.

Staff skins v2 are fixed to canonical rigs and production templates: Tuning Mechanic shirt/pants <code>117675305396899/82913293835329</code>; Service Helper <code>113380317733292/90839955712839</code>; Service Advisor <code>70547653911844/82986950506206</code>; Salesman shirt <code>86698594140963</code> with existing pants <code>93060083603617</code>; Painter <code>140096941026666/90654542542148</code>; Operations Manager <code>135795599598880/114232687232830</code>; Checkout Clerk <code>120137744642139/108400556770599</code>; Body Specialist <code>104282256106623/86570691878081</code>. Core runtime staff additionally changes outfits via <code>SalesmanService.FIXED_UNIFORMS</code>; Mechanics retain the approved v1 uniform, as the v2 package does not include a Mechanic set.

Folders such as <code>TestDriveMapRemoved_20260806</code>, <code>EnvironmentOverlapCleanup_*</code>, <code>SeparatedPropLeftovers_*</code> and plugin storage — are archive/operational remnants. Do not create runtime dependencies on them.

## 14. Local workspace and web-hub

The workspace root contains several projects and archives. Do not consider it a single runtime source tree.

### Canonical local areas

| Path | Purpose | Status |
|---|---|---|
| <code>sell_cars_source_recovered/</code> | current restored implementation of web-hub, assets, API compatibility | web-hub canon |
| <code>sell_weird_pizzas_hub/</code> | dirty old Git worktree; late/unreleased docs, tools, Rex/content assets and 78-pad source | artifact-specific reference, not deploy canon |
| <code>sell_weird_pizzas_hub/sell-cars-hud/</code> | five Workshop UI concepts, approved concept 3, generated transparent icons and local Luau source <code>roblox/SellCarsHUDClient.client.luau</code> | visual/source companion to current HUD draft; Studio remains runtime truth |
| <code>wiki/</code> | Castle Defense/GASC wiki | different project |
| <code>Assets/UI</code>, <code>ui_assets</code>, <code>GASC_ui</code> | primarily GASC/Castle Defense prototypes | not Sell Cars runtime |
| <code>higgsfield_bot/</code> and generated media | auxiliary image/content pipeline | support only |
| <code>_mirror/</code> | HTTP/static snapshot | archive, not source |
| <code>Roblox_BloomLabs/purchase-pad-order-editor.html</code> | offline drag/drop editor of normalized list <code>TycoonConfig.PADS</code>, dependency warnings and CSV all-active/by-stage | support tool; schema 3/storage v4 snapshot matches live editorial order, but the HTML itself does not mutate runtime |
| <code>codebase/</code> | local Lua/DataModel source snapshot | reference for reading and review; actual Roblox Studio reads/edits/tests are performed only through Roblox Studio MCP |

<code>codebase/</code> contains a local Luau snapshot shared/server/client, <code>StarterGui.SellCarsHUD</code> and centralized runtime packages, but is not a transport of changes into Studio. Studio remains the runtime and publish boundary. The canonical operational path — <code>Roblox Studio MCP: list_roblox_studios → get_studio_state → script_read/search/grep → multi_edit → start_stop_play → console/output assertions</code>.

### Web-hub implementation

<code>sell_cars_source_recovered</code>:

- vanilla static app without framework/bundler;
- <code>index.html</code> — main shell;
- <code>hub.html</code> — older alternate shell;
- <code>app.js</code> — data + renderers + hash router;
- <code>styles.css</code>;
- <code>api/protected.js</code> — restored compatible serverless implementation, not the original recovered backend source;
- function bundle <code>includeFiles</code> currently explicitly lists <code>index.html</code>, <code>app.js</code>, <code>styles.css</code>, <code>meme-pr.js</code>, <code>meme-pr.css</code>;
- package/site version around v0.14.1;
- Vercel project is linked to the public Sell Cars hub deployment.

Main pages: MVP, Pads, Cars, Tuning, Progression/Map, Gameplay Loop, Meme PR, Concepts, References.

A separate clickable Workshop prototype is published at <code>https://roblox.danilovx.com/sell-cars-ui/</code>. It is needed for comparing compositions and control behaviors, but is not an executable Roblox UI and does not replace Studio playtest.

Hub stores:

- product copy and roadmap;
- pad/car cards;
- concept galleries;
- reference archive;
- readiness/status board;
- admin screenshots;
- marketing/meme materials.

It **does not** store authoritative Roblox save, cash, pad purchases, or runtime scripts.

Within local hub sources there is another discrepancy:

- recovered/deployed <code>buyablePadGroups</code> shows **50 cards**: Junk 11, Repair 15, Tuning 16, Car Wash 8;
- <code>sell_weird_pizzas_hub/assets/docs/sell-cars-purchase-pads-by-stage.md</code> and associated <code>purchasePadStages</code> describe **78 planned pads**: 18 + 16 + 13 + 31;
- the currently open Studio has **153 active purchase pads** from 159 authored rows with total cost of $8,432,300; the local pad-editor 142/136 relates to an old PlaceVersion 967.

These numbers answer different questions. Neither 50 hub cards nor 78 planned pads replace the live build order from section 5.

### Statuses and API

Statuses are normalized to:

- <code>todo</code>;
- <code>concept</code>;
- <code>progress</code>;
- <code>ready</code>.

Shared statuses and screenshots live in Vercel Blob. An authenticated user reads them, an admin writes or deletes them. Planner progress in browser localStorage is a local simulation and is not linked to Roblox progression.

Operational contract:

- required <code>BLOB_READ_WRITE_TOKEN</code> and <code>AUTH_COOKIE_SECRET</code>;
- admin credential: preferred <code>AUTH_ADMIN_SHA256</code>, fallback <code>AUTH_ADMIN</code>;
- team credentials when used: <code>AUTH_MIKE</code>, <code>AUTH_YELNUR</code>;
- site session cookie lasts about 14 days, admin cookie about 12 hours;
- status object: <code>pad-statuses/&lt;group&gt;/&lt;pad&gt;.json</code>;
- legacy monolith <code>pad-statuses/statuses.json</code> is still read;
- car statuses use logical group <code>cars</code>;
- screenshot object: <code>pad-screenshots/&lt;group&gt;/&lt;pad&gt;/...</code>.

Status JSON and screenshot blobs are written to Blob <code>access: public</code>. UI/list APIs are closed to login, but direct blob URLs are not private storage. Never upload secrets, personal, or confidential materials there.

Do not publish values from <code>.env.local</code> or source fallbacks. The backend has security debt around fallback credentials/secrets; production must define strong env values.

### JPEG-only policy

Required rules from <code>sell_cars_source_recovered/AGENTS.md</code>:

1. Published raster concept art — only JPEG <code>.jpg</code>.
2. Generated PNG is first converted, then used.
3. Never delete <code>assets/**/*.png</code> from <code>.vercelignore</code>.
4. Admin screenshot uploader remains JPEG-only.
5. After change, verify all raster references in <code>app.js</code>.
6. Names — lowercase kebab-case, semantic folder.
7. <code>.vercelignore</code> intentionally excludes <code>assets/concepts/vehicle-lineup/toyoda-comet.jpg</code>; do not return this asset without a separate product/deploy solution.

SVG placeholders are acceptable. Marketing video may use MP4 and JPEG poster.

### Local preview

From <code>sell_cars_source_recovered</code> a static preview can be launched:

<code>python -m http.server 8000</code>

This checks shell/assets/router, but does not include full auth/API/Blob operations. <code>package.json</code> has no scripts, build pipeline, or test suite; do not invent non-existent <code>npm test</code>.

Dependency <code>@vercel/blob</code> is set to <code>latest</code>, lockfile is missing. Serverless dependency resolution is non-reproducible until version is pinned and lockfile is added. Static Python preview dependency install is not required, but API is not verified.

### Safe hub content change

1. Resolve, described as runtime or target concept.
2. Add JPEG to the correct <code>assets/concepts/&lt;domain&gt;/</code>.
3. Update the required data block in <code>app.js</code>; preserve RU/EN and stable title/slug.
4. For a new page, add nav/mount/renderer/styles.
5. Consistently bump site/package/cache-buster versions.
6. Verify existence of all assets, absence of raster PNG refs, hash/deep links, filters, lightbox, and responsive.
7. On serverless preview check login/logout, status read/write/reload, JPEG upload/delete and rejection of unsupported types.
8. If a new root JS/CSS dependency is added, verify <code>api/protected.js</code> <code>includeFiles</code>; otherwise the file may not be included in the function bundle.
9. After deploy perform smoke-test on authenticated root and explicitly public routes.

Catch-all app routes go through protected API, but <code>/assets/*</code>, <code>/GASC_ui/*</code>, <code>/hair-gallery/*</code> and <code>/marketplace-hair-gallery/*</code> bypass login. The latter galleries are unrelated auxiliaries, not Sell Cars canon. Do not describe the entire deployment as private.

Renaming title may change slug/Blob key. <code>statusAliases</code> helps only with car status lookup. For pad status and any screenshot paths, real Blob-key/data migration is required, otherwise the history will disappear.

### What in hub folders is not part of Roblox runtime

- <code>_mirror/</code>;
- <code>assets/screenshots/</code>, <code>assets/teardown-insights/</code>, <code>assets/qa/</code>;
- Sell Lemons docs/screenshots and historical Pizza concepts;
- <code>GASC_ui/</code>, hair galleries;
- Meme PR assets — part of marketing board, not gameplay source;
- in dirty original: <code>generated/</code>, <code>tools/rex_redline_tts/</code>, Rex concept/docs, <code>assets/ui/</code>, <code>assets/vfx/</code>.

These materials may be useful as reference, marketing or content pipeline, but do not prove the existence of a feature in place.

## 15. Runtime truth versus deployed/recovered hub concept

Recovered hub v0.14.1 is newer than parts of historical gameplay docs as the published web implementation, but chronological leadership of its product decisions is unproven. Its model fundamentally does not align with current Studio. Therefore, the project now has two clearly signed axes:

- **[LIVE] Current playable:** dealer stock is purchased, repaired, listed, and sold permanently; individual personal cars remain in permanent owner collection and do not generate passive business income.
- **[CONCEPT SNAPSHOT] Deployed/recovered hub v0.14.1:** permanent display cars that remain on the plot and generate passive income; repair improves earnings; calls and a short Main Money Track add activity.

The product owner must separately confirm whether this snapshot is the future target before AI begins migrating the live core loop.

### Table of discrepancies

| Topic | Runtime today | Deployed/recovered hub snapshot |
|---|---|---|
| Economic core | buy → repair → list → sell-away | permanent display + passive income |
| Buyer | physical walk-up buyer | calls as short trade |
| Phone | legacy fence sourcing up to FirstSalesman + separate post-tutorial personal-car desktop/market | optional deal on top of passive income |
| Main Money Track | gameplay is absent | short forgiving pickup loop |
| Car Wash | dual-source queues: Street/StationCash + Owner/CarValue; owner stage removes dirt | improvement of display car/earnings |
| Tuning/Paint | Dyno/Body/Paint dual-source queues; owner stages multiply future sale value, Street preserves PAINT + FINISH flow | hub directly removes paint mechanic and detailed tuning/part stats — clear conflict |
| MVP scope | already Wash1/Wash2/Tuning | Wash/Tuning were described later |
| Car lineup | 11 index cars: 4 low, 4 mid, 2 high, 1 hyper; all 11 dealer-eligible by progression gates, Zentauri gets 0.5% class share only upon full service; mid wash slots are empty | hub MVP1: Wronkler Rust 4x4, Rado Boxlet, Nissho Slide SX, Bavora B3; dozens of other cars only on broader board |
| Retention | capped offline base Cash after 90s, max 8h; return-popup | 2× Robux boost, currently only a safe UI placeholder without product/receipt |
| Endgame | TA_Compressor, chapter 6 | Premium → Tokyo → Alien → trophy |
| Ask More | deterministic increase with bonus | must have clear risk |

### Concept-snapshot macro-roadmap

Hub shows direction:

<code>Junk Lot → Repair Bay → Phone + Main Track → Car Wash + Tuning → Premium Showroom → Tokyo Drift Family HQ → Alien Crew Garage → Alien Vehicle Hangar</code>.

Executive Sky Garage — optional trophy, not rebirth/reset.

### Decision rule

If the task involves bug fix, preservation, current UX, or live economy — preserve the runtime loop.

If the task requests 'bringing the game to concept', 'passive displays', Main Track, Premium/Tokyo/Alien — first freeze the migration design:

1. What happens to current dealer stock and saved W/P slots.
2. How value and Cash economy are converted.
3. What remains of the physical buyer loop.
4. How the tutorial changes.
5. How profiles and analytics migrate.
6. Which hub cards become realized.

It is not possible to silently merge two models via local change of a single formula.

## 17. Safe workflow for AI

### Before any Studio change

1. Read this Bible: minimum header, thematic LIVE section, known risks, workflow and latest journal entries; do not rely on memory of old chat.
2. Use Roblox only through Roblox Studio MCP. Do not run local sync servers, do not attach sync plugins, and do not manage Studio via desktop UI automation.
3. Through <code>list_roblox_studios</code> select the exact instance, then confirm the current place name, PlaceId, and Edit mode via MCP state; on acceptance 2.68 this is <code>Sell Cars</code>, PlaceId <code>104040320298951</code>.
4. Cross-check PlaceVersion and DataStore name with the header of this file.
5. Determine the task status: LIVE bug, TARGET feature, or LEGACY cleanup.
6. Through Studio MCP find config, producer, consumer, remote, and persistence path.
7. Read the full affected function/module, not just one search snippet.
8. Check for identical copies in eight tycoon or car models.
9. Freeze invariants: ownership, exact trade/slot identity, payout authority, migration.

### Making a change

1. Make minimal, precise source edits; for multiple replacements use atomic multi-edit.
2. Do not use ad-hoc Lua execution as a way to 'save' code.
3. First modify the server contract, then shared DTO/config, then client presentation.
4. If an ID changes, add a migration/alias in the same change set.
5. If the physical model path changes, check all eight plots.
6. If the car chassis changes, synchronize all canonical copies.
7. Do not edit dated backups instead of the live source.

### Playtest

Before Play:

- use a test account or a copy of the place;
- be aware that Studio can write to the current DataStore;
- during economic testing, use a separate store namespace only as a deliberate test migration;
- record the starting profile/cash.

Minimal smoke test of current runtime:

1. fresh join and claim a plot;
2. two free pad and Cash Register;
3. one-car tutorial with Rusty, 9 Rex cues, rolling rejoin and fixed $1,000;
4. manual repair strictly Engine → Wheels, visual missing/restored front-left tyre;
5. initial wallet assertion <code>$3,300 − $100 − $25 − $6 − $4 − $1,500 = $1,665</code>, hidden until Ready Salesman pad and physical FirstSalesman W→P;
6. absence of player entry path in all dealer cars; first buyer transaction manually performed, after tutorial Accept/Ask More/Refuse and auto-accept;
7. progression-weighted world seller across all six tiers, including 0% hyper without full service and 0.5% with full service; separately verify Luck 1–5: reweight only unlocked classes, buyer HIGH CLASS/SUPER RICH, rarity offer bonus, and preservation of incoming paint/tint/body/neon variants;
8. phone fence and Tow selection through the same weighted pool and same persisted Luck;
9. FirstMechanic/FirstSalesman automation and exact pool <code>ServiceHelper/2/3</code> without double assignment of one <code>escortId</code>;

11. Repair Bay customer and manual checkout;
12. both Wash lanes;

Dyno, Body, Paint manual/auto work; Service Advisor auto-checkout +20% speed and Operations Manager ×1.25 Street payout; full payouts <code>$89,375 / $95,214 / $109,688</code> must remain above Repair <code>$80,450</code>, and current capsule-bonus drift <code>+$67,375 / +$70,214 / +$79,688</code> below Repair <code>+$79,850</code> must be explicitly accepted or rebalanced by a separate solution;

15. mixed Owner/Street dispatch: FIFO, burst cap 2, street 30s queue timeout, owner occupancy wait without listing abort, 90s manual-stall timeout, ×0.75 stall fallback and queued-stage restore after leave/rejoin; helper drives W→Repair Bay and the bay does not start work until that drive-in finishes;

Body Paint/Window Tint: locked/unlocked; body palette missing on unsupported models; for Fierro/Bavora/Chevrolux model-specific visible factory ids checked by $5,000, universal Black/White/Dark Red/Emerald/NARDO GREY/Midnight Blue by $25,000, red/gray factory suppression, exact-color/material preservation, first-purchase Cash delta, free owned reselect, hidden/cross-model ID rejection, insufficient Cash, Return/respawn and leave/rejoin; tint separately saves levels 0/50/100, black plus minimum two paid colors and optical-black 100;
24. mobile/console UI, if input is affected;

After testing stop Play, ensure Studio is back in Edit mode, check console and re-read the modified source;

### Historical acceptance 2.67 — initial Personal Garage Body Paint/Window Tint acceptance (superseded by 2.68)

The test was conducted in a precisely selected Studio <code>Sell Cars</code>, PlaceId <code>104040320298951</code>, on a physical active <code>Porschka911</code>. ### Economic validation

For any economic adjustment, separately verify:

- tutorial cash minimum/maximum;
- affordability of the next mandatory pad;
- revenue from selling car parts as anti-softlock;
- payout from all service stations at each multiplier step;
- automation in case of insufficient Cash;
- frozen listing price after reload;
- duplicate trade settlement;
- crash/leave save boundary.

The old <code>sell_weird_pizzas_hub/tools/validate_sell_cars_mvp_economy.py</code> models the historical build. It is not a test of current runtime and must be rewritten before use as a gate.

### Web-hub QA

- all referenced assets exist;
- raster concepts are only JPEG;
- no random <code>.png</code> refs;
- RU/EN copy does not diverge;
- deep links and hash router work;
- status counts, filters, lightbox, responsive;
- auth/session/status screenshot API verified on serverless preview;
- secrets not present in commit or output;
- version/cache busters are aligned.

## 18. Known defects and technical debt

### P0/P1 — fix before system expansion

1. **No cross-server profile lock:** risk of last-writer-wins overwrite.
2. **Not all durable station/repair mutations are immediately dirty:** crash-loss window.
3. **[RISK] Tuning-vs-Repair bonus regression:** full Tuning payouts are higher than Repair, but all three stage-capsule bonuses are below Repair +$79,850; balance in the current change set was intentionally not adjusted.
4. **Remaining legacy dead clicks:** old <code>Badges</code> and <code>Colorize</code> still point to missing <code>/PlayerGui/Misc/*Menu</code>. Workshop gear/settings already has its own working modal and this item no longer applies.

Closed 25 August: round-robin/fake seller rarity readout replaced with progression-weighted selector and exact <code>% ARRIVAL</code>; <code>ZentauriR</code> is now dealer-eligible with fullService share 0.5%. These items remain in old journal/research snapshots only as dated history.

### P2 — important limitations/traps

5. <code>RepairPart</code> remote is not usable.
6. <code>ListCar</code> — legacy refresh-only remote without client sender; ordinary W-listing commit performs <code>ListingTransport</code>, owner-pipeline listing — separate critical server transfer with subsequent server-owned physical gate→P leg.
7. Edit DataModel shows not all runtime remotes/events.
8. Tutorial Rush is missing, despite save fields.
9. On full load failure, a temporary blank profile with inconsistently secured economy paths appears.
10. All-plots-full has no normal UX.
11. Large monolithic modules and eight heavy plot copies increase performance regression risk. Interest management 800/950 studs reduces client service load, but eight-client soak after helper expansion is not completed; reported lag cannot be considered closed by a single static check or a single smoke Play.
12. Central vehicle runtime still creates a separate worker package for attached full rig; a unified passive-FX scheduler has not been separated and remains a subsequent performance-boundary.
13. No automated runtime test suite; current deterministic/synthetic harnesses and isolated Play are point-in-time, not continuous CI acceptance.
14. Comments and config contain dormant economy rules that are easily mistaken for live.
15. Web-hub product copy conflicts with runtime. Current version drift specifically: <code>SITE_VERSION</code> and package — 0.14.1, <code>index.html</code> cache-busters — 0.13.0, <code>hub.html</code> — 0.12.0.
16. Web-hub serverless dependency <code>@vercel/blob</code> uses <code>latest</code> without a lockfile.
17. Between service stages, client-local owner car proxy is still recreated, but queue clone of the same <code>tradeId</code> returns a live CFrame helper and is destroyed. Junk→Repair Bay travels via queue clone, service-run — only driveway; catch-up no longer holds lift while the vehicle is still on the road. This is visually continuous, but there is still no single continuous physical Model instance.
18. Queue → W duplicate Junk Spot proxy is closed in 2.45: one Model maintains <code>DealerProxyId</code> and visual condition through promotion and buy boundary. A separate suspicion of extra parking alignment outside this boundary remains an active investigation until exact route snapshot is available.
19. Workshop HUD 2.7.1 is compile-checked in Edit, but seat-aware hiding/revealing of the lower arrow after full drive to Used Cars, phone long-copy layout, and all modal click-through contracts requires a single targeted PC/mobile runtime smoke. Until then, this UI wave should not be marked as fully accepted or published.

### Not implemented, but easily mistaken for a feature

- Main Money Track reward loop;
- passive display income;
- Premium/Tokyo/Alien gameplay;
- relationship/served-count buyer resolver exceeds already working wealth/rarity multipliers;
- Rush;
- rebirth/prestige;
- monetization.

## 19. Debug/admin [LIVE]

Debug UI opens via screen button/F8 and calls server RemoteFunctions. After playtest security pass, debug tools and chat commands must go through server-side authorization; <code>/addmoney &lt;username&gt; [amount]</code> and <code>/releasecar &lt;username&gt; [tradeId|all]</code> are admin-only. The old solution <code>isAuthorized → true</code> for all players is considered superseded and is not a production contract. Server action cooldown remains enabled.

Debug-panel allowlist is separated from chat-admin allowlist. <code>Kukrill</code> has access to OWNER DEBUG and its re-verified server actions, but this alone does not authorize individual chat commands <code>/addmoney</code> and <code>/releasecar</code>. Client does not duplicate the list and does not make membership decisions: it creates the panel only after <code>GetState.authorized=true</code> from the server. Upon entering Seat, an already-opened large panel closes, but the compact owner button <code>DEBUG</code> remains visible and allows reopening the panel directly in the vehicle; server authorization does not change.

In <code>OWNER DEBUG → SPAWN CAR</code> after successful spawn, a compact <code>FactoryColorOptions</code> is revealed only if <code>VehiclePaint.FACTORY_PALETTES[carId]</code> actually exists. Currently, swatches are available for Bavora, ChevroluxC10, and FierroRossa. Spawn maintains a server-side reference and a separate <code>DebugInstanceId</code> for the selected debug vehicle; <code>SetDebugCarColor</code> revalidates the owner, selected instance, and palette ID, after which it repaints the paint targets on the existing Model. The color action does not clone, delete, spawn, or call <code>PivotTo</code>. Clear removes the selection along with the debug cars.

Available actions:

- AddMoney / RemoveMoney;
- SpawnCar;
- UndoLastPad;
- ClearDebugCars;
- SetClock / SetWeather;
- ResetProfile.

<code>ResetProfile</code> performs a critical save and kicks the user. This is a destructive operation: check the user, place, and test environment before calling it. Debug-spawned cars are restricted and should not enter the normal save/trade lifecycle.

Hideable service-route overlays live in a sibling folder <code>Workspace.ServiceRouteDebug</code>, <strong>Tycoon1 only</strong>, not inside <code>ServiceRouteMarkers</code>. This is only heavy editor visualization; authoritative runtime data resides in separate <code>ServiceRouteMarkers.Tycoon1…Tycoon8</code>. Wash P1–P4 and Repair/Tuning P1–P7 now exist for all eight plots and are live path sources with matching code fallbacks. Debug draws <strong>on-lot only</strong> control points for RepairBay1/2, CarWash1/2, TuningDyno/Body/Paint, and short same-plot hops Tuning→Repair / Repair→Used Cars / Tuning→Used Cars. Tuning has separate <code>Inbound</code> and <code>Outbound</code> vehicle lines from the road frontage through Entry/Stop and back through Exit/RoadExit; with 1.47 a subfolder <code>Staff</code> from WorkerHome is also drawn along the entire WorkPath. Neon balls sit on projected ramp/street Y (not editor Y≈3), <code>CanQuery=true</code> so Studio can select them.

1.42 first pass placed T2–8 <code>WashStart</code>/<code>WashEnd</code> at Tycoon1 BrushMachine WorldPivots and added RoadGraph inbound hops into the north street gap; beams 600–1100 studs crossed the city / Karlosh ring. 1.43 was rebuilt from each lane’s DriveSurface, 1.44 temporarily removed T2–8 route folders, and 2.19 finally closed the original world-geometry debt: Buildings were reassembled with transformation of nested <code>Model.WorldPivot</code>, brush distance became 2.420 studs on all plots, per-plot <code>ServiceRouteMarkers</code> was restored, and runtime/debug guard drops pivots beyond 40 studs. <code>ServiceRouteDebug</code> is intentionally Tycoon1-only and is not a runtime dependency.

One hide switch: BoolValue <code>Visible</code> default true, attribute <code>DebugVisible=true</code>, or Explorer-hide the folder. Play: <code>StarterPlayer.StarterPlayerScripts.ServiceRouteDebugToggle</code>. Rebuild: <code>ServerStorage.ServiceRouteDebugBuilder</code>. Text: <code>ServiceRouteDebug.HOW_TO_HIDE</code>.

## 20. Definition of Done

### For Roblox gameplay change

Change is ready only if:

- behavior matches LIVE or explicitly approved TARGET solution;
- server authority is preserved;
- remotes validate type, ownership, identity, state, and rate;
- save schema/migration/rollback has been considered;
- all plot/model copies are consistent;
- fresh, restore, and insufficient-funds paths have been verified;
- tutorial and next objective are not broken;
- mobile/console has been considered;
- console is clean;
- this file is updated if rules, paths, store, pad list, or known risks have changed.

### For web-hub change

- content source is correctly tagged as LIVE/TARGET/LEGACY;
- JPEG policy is followed;
- all asset references exist;
- RU/EN and deep links work;
- shared status keys are not lost;
- auth/API are verified where applicable;
- versions/cache busters are aligned;
- secrets are not present in source/output.

## 21. Mini glossary

| Term | Value |
|---|---|
| Dealer car | stock that the player buys, repairs, and resells |
| Personal car | permanent collection car from Personal Garage: managed only by the owner and has one shared passenger seat; not dealer W/P stock or customer service car |
| Customer car | external NPC vehicle in service queue; not player-owned |
| Owner pipeline | persistent route for dealer car: critical W→processingCars, Repair, mandatory wash of dirty stock, and all built Tuning stages, then critical processingCars→P/listing |
| StationCash / CarValue | mutually exclusive reward modes: immediate payout from customer job or multiplier for future price of owned stock |
| W slot | working/repair slot W1 or W2 |
| P slot | display/sale slot P1…P6 |
| Pad | purchase trigger and progression record |
| Bundle | multiple physical build members, atomically purchased via one leader pad |
| Merged pad | authored row, hidden/merged into another purchase |
| Listing | price fixation; standard stock goes through physical W→P, completed via owner pipeline — critical processing→P transfer |
| Trade ID | server identity of a specific transaction; basis of idempotency |
| Fallback offer | current built-in TradeContract resolver |
| Parts Sales Laptop / Selling Car Parts | repeatable anti-softlock station income: manual part sales after FirstMechanic — automatic thereafter |
| Hub | web catalog concept/status/assets; not Roblox runtime |
| Season | version of DataStore namespace |

---

## 22. Contract for this Bible's maintenance

After significant changes, update:

1. the audit date, PlaceVersion, and DataStore in the header;
2. the LIVE behavior section;
3. the vehicle, pad, payout, and remote tables;
4. the file map;
5. migrations and known risks;
6. conflict log between runtime and target concept;
7. a brief summary below.

### How to cross-check the journal with the baseline

The journal — a chronology of change sets — is not an independent source of current truth: a newer entry may supersede an older one, and a brief description may mix implemented, tested, deferred, and only noted risks. When transferring an entry into the main canon, find the executable producer/consumer in the current Studio code, update the thematic section, and separately save the verification limit. If a journal claim is not confirmed by current code or by later runtime acceptance, it remains dated evidence or an active investigation and is not marked <code>[LIVE]</code> as established fact.

The cross-check 2.44–2.50 from 27 August solidified the current superseding contract: one Rusty, 9 tutorial cues, fixed $1,000, FirstSalesman to W→P and the first manual buyer transaction; all dealer stock is staff-only. Three tutorial vehicles, early Zentauri, player-driven/manual listing, and Quick Fix remain only as history. The rest of the confirmed contracts — Luck/weighted arrivals, a pool of three helper scripts, anti-softlock watchdog, service interest management, Nitro debug-only, W→P concurrency, and route/deadlock fixes — are preserved in thematic sections. Unconfirmed remain excessive parking alignment and the absence of a full eight-client performance soak.
