## 23. Product audit of semantic integrity and re-playability [LIVE/RISK]

Primary runtime audit, competitive references, and calculations are located in [<code>TYCOON_REPLAYABILITY_RESEARCH_2026-08-17.md</code>](TYCOON_REPLAYABILITY_RESEARCH_2026-08-17.md). A dated reconstruction of the internal vision and second roadmap are located in [<code>BLOOM_BRAIN_SELL_CARS_RESEARCH_2026-08-18.md</code>](BLOOM_BRAIN_SELL_CARS_RESEARCH_2026-08-18.md). This section contains only the canonical summary required prior to further changes to the game.

### Status of Solutions

- The primary runtime audit confirmed Source/DataModel PlaceVersion 1179 without launching Play.
- Read-only cross-check 18 August on PlaceVersion 1198 reconfirmed 144/138 pads and $8,346,250, cyclic stock selector, absence of owner-stock channel Zentauri, early terminal, $7m staff, and ghost UI. This is a dated audit snapshot: conclusions about selector/Zentauri and counts **[SUPERSEDED 2026-08-25]**. Cross-audit did not launch or stop Play.
- A fresh TycoonConfig recount of the current unpublished draft confirms **159 authored / 153 active pads** with a total value of **$8,432,300**. All sourcing channels use weighted selector, and Zentauri has a full-service owner-stock share of 0.5%. Previous aggregate figures remain only as dated snapshots.
- Bloom Brain was checked against KB snapshot <code>902d96344787334ae3a8295daf661f08497a73cd</code>. All used Sell Cars notes and teardowns have the status <code>needs-review</code>; this is evidence of the design history, not an approved specification.
- Product proposals are marked as **[UNKNOWN] / hypothesis**; they do not become TARGET without a owner's resolution.
- Neither study modified Studio, DataStore, runtime scripts, assets, balance, or shared Bloom Brain KB; <code>commit_note</code> was not called.
- A clean playtest for the primary audit was deliberately not launched: Studio uses the real DataStore season and can record profile/autosave.

### Main Diagnosis

A strong Sell Cars core — the physical story of a specific vehicle: the dealer arrives, the player makes a purchase decision and manually interacts with repairs on the first Rusty, then staff physically moves dealer stock through W→P and built services, and the buyer comes to pick up a specific vehicle. The player manually closes the first sale and then manages an automated business. This identity does not need to be replaced with generic tycoon-meta.

The main deficiency is not a lack of content, but a lack of solutions and memory:

1. Almost no error is possible before the deal.
2. After automation, the player loses actions but does not gain a policy/management layer.
3. After the sale, the vehicle's history disappears.
4. After Compressor, there is no new horizon, although most of the price has not yet been purchased.
5. Other players exist nearby, but almost do not change the task.

### Bloom Brain cross-audit: internal vision versus runtime

1. **Driving-first is partially closed in Personal Garage + MainTrack activity.** Dealer W→P and manual takeover remain staff-only, but permanent personal cars now allow owner-only driving, one shared passenger seat, Neon/Nitro customization, and a repeatable economic MainTrack run: direction-aware arch-gated one loop, 24 route positions, rolling window of three horizontal-footprint pickups, successful finish at minimum 12 collections and 10-minute persisted cooldown. Reward is awarded only for collected points and consumes market price personal car and business-income multiplier. This is no longer just a cosmetic layer, yet there is still no direct proof/readout for <code>speed/handling/acceleration</code>, certification, or buyer brief.
2. **<code>ASK MORE</code> — product-decision debt, not just a simple implementation drift.** Huddle 6 August proposed that the player always ask for approximately 12% more. Runtime closely implements this weak rule, so the negotiation contract needs to be adjusted: limiting moves, readable temperament, and real walk-away possibility.
3. **The recorded pricing proposal is not implemented.** In the same <code>needs-review</code> huddle, it was proposed that the player list prices approximately 15% above calculated value, and the buyer propose 85–105% listing. Live calculates listed price automatically, and the standard fallback starts at 92% and then deterministically applies wealth/rarity multipliers; independent dealer decision and buyer bid range still did not reach runtime.
4. **Rarity-gap is closed at the base level; tuning autonomy is closed only for personal cars.** The tutorial contains only Rusty; scripted early Zentauri is no longer present. After the tutorial, all sourcing channels use progression-weighted random arrivals and show actual <code>% ARRIVAL</code>, and Zentauri appears only after full service unlock with exact 0.5% class share. Personal Garage now allows owners to save body color and tint level/color choices; dealer owner-pipeline Paint/Body still selects system roll/stage result. Open questions remain: pity/streak smoothing, speed of class mix improvement, and whether similar autonomy is needed for dealer builds.
5. **Tuning — documented scope expansion.** 6 August Tuning Atelier was post-MVP, 14 August it had already entered the main value chain, and by 17 August paint/neon/staff presentation were deeply implemented. In found records, there is no separate decision record explaining the shift in priority until proven appraisal, negotiation, and meaningful driving.
6. **Fixed day — an intentional defer, not a forgotten defect.** 10 August the team explicitly chose daytime lighting for visibility and deferred weather/time-of-day. This cannot be prioritized alongside false probability, dead clicks, or missing pricing step.
7. **No approved Sell Cars endgame/social/D8–28 plan found.** These ideas appear in practitioner heuristics and competitor teardowns, but the current set of relevant records has <code>needs-review</code>. Weekly contracts, co-op, specializations, and trophy memory remain hypotheses until resolved by the owner.

Preferred dependency for next development: <code>truth + funnels → честная первая сделка → appraisal/pricing/buyer brief/service choice → Salesman policies → stat-driven расширение live MainTrack + Sold Car Book → weekly/co-op/endgame</code>. Do not mask the absence of choice with a new number of pads or LiveOps content.

### Current semantic gaps and closed items

| Player promise | Live contract | Consequence |
|---|---|---|
| Rare find **[CLOSED 2026-08-25]** | <code>selectArrival</code> applies class weights by progression, Luck reweight only within unlocked set and <code>spawnWeight</code> within class; seller shows actual <code>% ARRIVAL</code> profile | Random-arrival promise is now honest; the need for pity/streak smoothing and the source of Luck improvement remain separate product choices |
| Evaluate purchase value | Even with worst seller ask after three parts, each dealer car still yields positive profit | <code>BUY</code> almost always dominates over <code>REFUSE</code>; fantasy dealer does not require appraisal |
| Bargain with buyer | <code>ASK MORE</code> with bonus increases offer without risk of loss; with zero bonus, buyer also does not leave | The button is a required optimization, not a margin/speed risk |
| NPC behavior affects deal **[PARTIALLY CLOSED 2026-08-26]** | Budget affects eligibility; <code>offerMultiplier</code> now actually provides POOR/BUYER/FENCE/HIGH CLASS/SUPER RICH multipliers, rarity adds 0–25%, askMore/haggleChance are read. <code>served</code> still does not change fallback or relationships | Buyer class and rarity now affect payout, but persistent relationship remains only a presentation promise |
| Tuning — player decision **[PARTIALLY CLOSED 2026-08-30]** | Personal Garage body/tint is now selected and saved by the player; dealer service stages, paint hue, and neon color are still chosen via rolls/system | Autonomy has appeared for permanent personal cars, but dealer Signature Build/packages remain open product decisions |
| Car already repaired **[CLOSED 2026-08-18]** | Repair enters owner plan only when persisted <code>oilLeak=true</code>; Wash — only when <code>dirty=true</code> | A clean/repairs car no longer receives a false mandatory service stage |
| Sell Cars remains a flip game | Full Paint customer job yields $109,688; full dealer flip Porschka at worst ask yields about $57,582 profit | Late rational loop becomes a service factory, not a car dealing activity |
| BUSINESS COMPLETE | Compressor still completes the guided chain until purchase of a costly optional Admin branch; fresh full active total is 153 pads/$8,432,300, and the exact guided/post-completion split after RB2/helper expansion requires a new recount | The terminal HUD may still disappear before the actual end of the most expensive branch |
| Building opens opportunities **[RECOUNT NEEDED]** | The old category ratio was calculated before expansion; current base contains 153 active pads, but the new semantic breakdown has not been executed | A semantic category recount must be repeated before citing the shell/multiplier pad share again |
| Hypercar unlock **[CLOSED 2026-08-25]** | <code>ZentauriR dealerEligible=true</code>; after both lifts, both brushes, and three Tuning stands, receives exact 0.5% class share across all sourcing channels | Car Index unlock now aligns with the real owner-stock flip channel |
| Vehicle stats are needed **[PARTIALLY CLOSED 2026-08-30]** | MainTrack now economically consumes personal car market price and business income; real vehicle speed naturally affects lap time, but reward does not read <code>topSpeedKph/handlingRating/accelerationRating</code> from Car Index | Driving/reward layer exists; clear mastery/certification proof of three readout stats remains an open product decision |
| Visible button works | Workshop Settings works; legacy Badges/Colorize still lead to missing menus, and 2× 49 R$ — honestly signed but visible placeholder | Remaining dead clicks and product promises reduce trust |
| Multiplayer tycoon | There are eight adjacent plots, but no co-op core action; the ninth player at full server does not get tycoon status | The Social scene does not create intentional co-play, and capacity leads to onboarding failure |

### Replayability: what exists and what is missing

Current variability exists: 11 dealer-eligible catalog cars, progression-weighted arrivals, 40 townsfolk, budgets/haggle, dirty/oil rolls, weather, radio, several queues and visual colors. Owner plan is deterministic based on built services and persisted conditions; randomness no longer allows selling a dirty car when a wash is available or skipping a purchased tuning.

Replayability should work across three horizons:

- **2–10 minutes:** vehicle condition, seller motivation, buyer brief, and capacity require a different solution within a familiar flip.
- **20–40 minutes:** the player chooses between a quick flip, restoration, service order, build goal, and social contract.
- **D8–28:** reputation/specialization, weekly choose-one contracts, collection history, city tender, and new types of tasks without losing previously built business.

Roblox evaluates Recommended For You starting from 15 June 2026 within a 28-day window, with separate D1, D2–7 and D8–28 buckets, first-play bounce, playtime/play days/qualified sessions, intentional co-play, and spend signals. The old description is only 7-day/qPTR temporarily outdated. Source: [Roblox Newsroom](https://about.roblox.com/newsroom/2026/06/optimizing-discovery-great-games-reach-millions-players-roblox) and [DevForum announcement](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/recommended-for-you-algorithm-improvements-that-better-value-long-term-retention/4684575).

### Preferred architecture — hypothesis, not TARGET

1. **Appraisal/inspection.** Before BUY, show symptoms, price range, and unknown risk; tools/personnel improve accuracy, not just payout.
2. **Honest negotiations.** Counter/hold/walk with readable temperament and limited moves; rejection should sometimes be correct.
3. **Procurement policies after Salesman.** Cash reserve, allowed class/source, minimum margin, queue capacity, and service strategy. Automation removes routine tasks but opens up control.
4. **Selectable service packages.** Quick Flip / Retail Ready / Signature Build with ETA, expected sale range, and queue occupancy.
5. **Market board.** Small transparent demand modifiers, fixed for already completed deals, to prevent stock devaluation retrospectively.
6. **Sold Car Book.** Before/after, margin, buyer, service plan, paint/neon, and photos. Personal Garage is a limited trophy showroom, not a passive replacement for the sell-away loop.
7. **Reputation and specializations without reset.** Volume Dealer / Restoration / Service Empire as horizontal branches with different contracts.
8. **Expansion of live MainTrack consumer.** The current arch-gated one-lap cash route is already a confirmed owner activity. The next hypothesis is to use the same track/session boundaries for test drive, certification, delivery, and buyer brief, linking results to <code>speed/handling/acceleration</code>, rather than building a second parallel track system.
9. **Weekly choose-one contracts.** Without streak loss; the task should be a milestone, not "repeat 30 times."
10. **Co-op flip pilot.** Clear permissions, roles inspect/repair/test-drive, and capped rewards; without transferring money or vehicles between players.

Do not add until core issues with pets, paid random cars, containers, second currency, battle pass, gifting/trading, player auctions, or mechanical rebirth are fixed. These will expand save/exploit/content surface but will not create a solution within a single vehicle.

### First, measurement

<code>TycoonBootstrap</code> initializes <code>GameAnalytics</code>; the current onboarding funnel is recorded as a specialized Creator Analytics funnel and simultaneously preserves custom events:

~~~text
FTUE:
TycoonClaimed → CashRegisterPurchased → FirstSellerArrived → FirstCarAcquired
→ FirstPartFixed → FirstRepairCompleted → FirstSalesmanHired
→ FirstCarListed → FirstBuyerOffer → FirstCarSold → TutorialCompleted

Progression:
FirstSale → CarJack → AirCompressor → FirstMechanic → RepairBay → Wash
→ Tuning → Compressor → FirstEndgameContract

Social:
InvitePrompt → InviteSent → FriendJoined → SharedAction → CoOpComplete
~~~

Key metrics: first-play bounce, time-to-first-offer/sale, drop-off by step, meaningful flips/session, margin distribution, D1/D2–7/D8–28, return after completion, and intentional co-play days. Monetary balance cannot be optimized solely by session length.

### Owner decisions that remain [UNKNOWN]

1. In endgame, does Sell Cars primarily lead to a dealership with a service portfolio or a service empire with rare flips?
2. Compressor means <code>OPERATIONAL</code>, actual <code>COMPLETE</code>, or completion of one specialization?
3. Should independent progression-weighted draws remain as-is or be enhanced with pity/streak smoothing; which class weights and transition speeds between tiers should pass the next balance playtest?
4. How severe are hidden defects and walk-away tolerance in a regular deal?
5. Personal Garage is confirmed as a permanent owned collection, and MainTrack as a repeatable one-lap reward consumer. The open question now is: does the collection receive trophy memory and stat-driven certification/delivery contracts without passive-income substitution of the dealer loop?
6. What co-op permissions are acceptable without a trading/gifting economy?
7. When the team is ready for a 2–4-week LiveOps cadence and only then for cosmetic-first monetization?

## 24. Sell Cars V2 UX/UI and Rusty FTUE [IMPLEMENTED IN OPEN STUDIO — 2026-08-31; SERVER-SCHEMA CLAIM SUPERSEDED 2026-09-01]

This contract is implemented only in the open Place **Sell Cars V2** (`placeId 130009689985995`). The old Start Place **Sell Cars** (`placeId 104040320298951`) is unchanged. Publishing V2 and switching the Start Place have not been performed: they remain separate steps after owner visual acceptance.

### Immutable gameplay contract

- The first transaction remains `Cash Register → Rusty → Engine → Wheels → Salesman → Buyer → Sale`.
- In this run, the map, server economy, DataStore/save schema, personnel, prices, remotes, and server states did not change. This is a historical boundary of the run 2026-08-31, not the current canon: section 25 below explicitly adds schema/remotes/server-state changes.
- Cash remains at the top center. `ObjectiveWorldTarget`, existing Beam/Highlight and server-authoritative pad touch remain the only owners of navigation and purchase; a new yellow path and confirmation-click were not added.
- Nine existing Rex cues are preserved. The cue waits for UI focus to be released; the tutorial cue is discarded if the objective has already changed.

### UI ownership

`ReplicatedStorage.UI` contains `ThemeTokens`, `UIModeService`, `FocusController` and a general `NegotiationPresenter`. Presenters remain separate, and the focus-controller selects exactly one mode by priority:

`World 10 → Notice 20 → PadPreview/Repair 30 → Phone 40 → Dialogue 50 → DealClosure/Receipt 60 → Cinematic 70`.

The controller mirrors compatibility attributes `MVPDealModal`, `SellCarsWorkshopModalOpen`, `MVPObjectiveSuppressedByModal`, `SellCarsWorldSuppressed` and `SellCarsRexSuppressed`. Dialogue/Closure hides Cash and competing HUD; Receipt restores Cash but temporarily hides Objective/Beam, notices, inspection cards, Phone and Rex.

### Implemented surfaces

- Objective is pinned in the top-right safe zone and displays one current action in a thin line 320×46 px on desktop. A progress bar/counter is not created; optional reward and `−/+` live in the same line. Cash is not moved from top-center.
- `PadHologramClient`: hysteresis `22/25 studs`, states `Dormant/Selected/Blocked`. A separate `PadWorldCard_*` is forbidden: the nearest selected pad temporarily expands its own authored billboard 150×56 → 190×82, preserves original `GothamBlack` title and `FredokaOne` price and adds only one large yellow authoritative line `TycoonConfig.padEffectText(definition)`. Category and self-evident action/status are not shown: Car Jack/Air Compressor must provide `+25% REPAIR SPEED`, without `REPAIR`, `STEP ON PAD TO BUY` and `SHORT $…`. On exit, the billboard returns to original size/layout, runtime effect is hidden. Purchase remains an existing action on the pad.
- After successful action `BuildAnimationClient` shows the same authoritative effect from a separate short rising/fading receipt: FredokaOne, solid green text, single black outline, fully transparent background. Capsule and colored UIStroke are forbidden for all characteristic bonuses.
- Seller and Buyer use one `NegotiationPresenter`: name, up to two lines of dialogue, greed bar, and only server-authorized actions. BUY/SELL are green, counter is yellow, walk is dark; tutorial whitelist preserves hidden Walk. All actions use `Activated`, primary selectable for controller. Buyer does not have the right to automatically select camera from another part of the plot: when distance to exact buyer exceeds `16 studs` offer remains in existing Phone; world Dialogue is only enabled when physically near the buyer.
- Dialogue Camera preserves CameraType/Subject/CFrame/FOV, controls and AutoRotate; after `0.46s` it transitions to a close frontal face shot. The primary camera candidate is located on a positive `HumanoidRootPart.LookVector` NPC at a distance of about `5.6 studs` with FOV `44`; minor frontal offsets are allowed only as raycast fallback when obstructed. A light edge vignette and local DepthOfField are preserved. Close/success/walk/death/NPC removal/CurrentCamera replacement restores state.
- Deal Closure begins only on confirmed hide/result payload, holds camera, uses local procedural right-shoulder pose R6/R15 with safe skip and displays a compact ownership/profit result without blocking server progress. For purchase, ownership-result is held `0.7s`, then camera and controls are returned without extra pause before seller departure; sale preserves a readable pause `1.1s`.
- Repair tracker is built from authoritative `CarState.workings[].parts` and authored `manualParts`. For Rusty, it shows only `Engine → Wheels`; regular cars support actual `Engine/Wheels/Body`. `RepairProgressPresenter` does not create a second world layer: it finds an existing server-authored `DealerValueBillboard`, expands it to 310×146, and adds it inside the same canvas caption `FIX <PART> • complete/total`, with orange→yellow→green progress and `CAR FIXED` hold `1.55s`; standard `CAR NAME / $VALUE / +$GAIN FROM REPAIRS` are repositioned below without overlap. Neither caption nor progress track/fill receives `UIScale`: at 100% a stationary strip briefly intensifies the green glow/3 px outline, and a separate high-contrast white-green `FinishSweep` passes from left to right over 0.85 s. A single transition snapshot without a working record does not destroy the card: `presenceToken` and grace `0.32s` preserve the same instance between `1/3 → 2/3 → 3/3`; the real end of repair still removes it after grace/finish hold. The card has no background panel/capsule/accent/gloss: the body remains visible behind floating text; contrast is ensured via Gotham identity/action, common money typography, text gradients/strokes, and the gradient progress track. Vertical spacing between caption/name/value/track/gain is equal to `0–27 / 27–54 / 54–89 / 94–110 / 116–142`. The Tutorial repair helper preserves exact Highlight/SelectionBox details, but its separate `TutorialRepairBillboard_*` is disabled. In compact hover state, the entire detail layer is hidden behind marker 24×24; a late-added progress-root is dynamically accounted for. The redundant screen `-$0 / +$...` repair-card is removed; value feedback remains as a floating `+$VALUE` from `CarVfxClient` with fully transparent background and zero cost.
- Sale receipt on the right is built solely from authoritative `ProfitCard`: `INVESTED = cost + serviceCost`, `SOLD = paid`, `PROFIT = payload.profit` (fallback `sold - invested`), and automatically closes without a continue button.

### Responsive contract and QA

Implementation and QA of any UI must occur in order <code>iPhone/mobile → tablet → desktop</code>; the profile list below is not a desktop-first order. One state/data logic uses separate layout profiles `PhoneLandscape`, `Portrait`, `Tablet`, `Desktop`, `CoreUISafeInsets`, touch targets 56 px, and a separate vertical rail for portrait. Routes for iPhone 7/17 landscape, Galaxy A06 landscape, portrait fallback, tablet, 1920×1080, ultrawide, and gamepad have been verified.

MCP Play QA in V2 confirmed: syntactic compilation of all new/modified scripts; single-focus suppression; Seller and Buyer visuals; top-center Cash + Rusty tracker `✓ ENGINE / ● WHEELS`; exact pad-card content; Scriptable camera + DoF and full restoration of FOV/AutoRotate/controls; absence of new warnings/errors from `SellCarsUXClient`, `NegotiationPresenter`, `PadHologramClient`, `ObjectiveClient`, `RexVoiceClient`, and `FocusController`. Studio is returned to Edit. Blank-profile destructive reset was not performed; UI contracts were additionally verified with synthetic client payloads without new remotes and without changes to server logic.

## 25. Sell Cars V2 Mobile Bug/UX pass — iPhone 13 [2026-09-01]

This mobile-first pass was executed only in the selected Studio **Sell Cars V2** (<code>placeId 130009689985995</code>). The report from iPhone 13 is the primary regression set. The recording segment after enabling <code>OWNER DEBUG</code> is not proof of balance.

### IMPLEMENTED

- **Starter sale/recovery.** Direct <code>Sell</code> is rejected. <code>PrepareSell</code> returns a one-use token with TTL 15 seconds, and <code>ConfirmSell</code> re-verifies ownership, token, occupancy/customize state, and sole-starter guard. A single free Rusty car cannot be sold; if another personal car exists, it is released for $0 only after explicit confirmation, and the UI displays $0 and the price of reverse purchase. Selling an active car within the same transaction assigns another owned car as active. Migration <code>starterRecoveryVersion=1</code> returns Rusty to the affected old profile with an empty collection and never repeats.
- **Personal Garage capacity recovery / exact-full replacement.** Invariant ownership — <code>ownedCount ≤ purchased physical garage bays</code>. Purchase in a free bay is standard; when exact-full/no-overflow is unlocked and an unowned car is being bought, it is only acquired as a clearly confirmed atomic replacement of the current active car via a one-use <code>PrepareReplacementPurchase → ConfirmReplacementPurchase</code> token with TTL 15 seconds. The server rechecks ownership/unlock/value/capacity/affordability, applies standard resale credit, and in one critical transaction saves the target as current; standalone sale of the sole car remains prohibited. Any legacy/future overflow is continuously computed by the server and after Offline Earnings opens a mandatory phone-first <code>GARAGE CAPACITY UPDATE</code>: X/raw Shade does not close the window, standard garage actions are blocked, the player sequentially selects excess vehicles. Each overflow sale uses a separate one-use <code>PrepareCapacitySell → ConfirmCapacitySell</code> token with TTL 15 seconds and rechecks overflow/ownership/value. Any owned car is permitted, including free Rusty/Wronkler, but the last allowable vehicle cannot be sold. Capacity refund: acquisition + current saved configuration + 10% goodwill; repeated historical recolor purchases are not summed, only the current/last finish is considered. Active/secondary assignments are normalized in the critical transaction, rollback returns cash, ownership, and customizations.
- **Negotiation.** Seller/Buyer use a single compact Workshop-language: without a large semi-transparent square overlay, with identical fonts/outlines, separate green/yellow/gray action surfaces, light gradients, and white text with dark stroke. <code>GREED</code> smoothly transitions fill from green → yellow → orange → red. On a new conversation, the scroll returns to the first line, buttons have a minimum 56 px. Dialogue hides RUN, DEBUG, competing prompts/billboards and does not close the NPC's head.
- **Inspection/Garage/offline cards.** PhoneLandscape car profile has a compact right card, placed below the live CashPanel and uses a transparent 56×56 tap-zone with a visible red <code>X</code> 30×30. Staff profile is reduced to 286×286 on iPhone 13 safe viewport and has a visible Close 38×38 within 56 px target. Personal Garage has a panel 494×274, viewport 474×170 and row 468×138; <code>SEND TO GARAGE</code>/<code>SPAWN CAR</code> — visual 116×38 within 120×56 targets, Close visual 34×34 within 56×56 target. Offline Earnings on phone — 520×208; amount and yellow away copy are on the same line, actions are in one row, <code>ALREADY ADDED TO YOUR BALANCE</code> is removed.
- **Focus/HUD.** <code>SprintController</code> — single owner RUN visibility: <code>MVPDetailsOpen</code>, <code>MVPDealModal</code>, <code>ActiveInspectionCard</code> and <code>SellCarsWorkshopModalOpen</code> hide RUN and reset held sprint; close restores it from current attributes, not from an outdated snapshot. Server-authorized <code>OWNER DEBUG</code>/F8 exists only in Studio or with an explicit unpublished QA flag and is additionally hidden during any of these focus states. Notifications do not use the lower joystick/jump/RUN zone; Track HUD lives in the activity rail, Rex is in a compact 64…72 px strip with 56 px close.
- **World labels/navigation.** Mobile tap-only and one selected card are preserved for inspection/deal/queue/decorative world cards. Priorities: active objective/repair → tapped object → available deal → queue → decorative; PhoneLandscape shows no more than two expanded cards. Canonical purchase-pad billboards, including Second Garage Bay, do not enter <code>WorldHoverClient</code>: their standard <code>Title/Price</code> remain visible under normal pad contract and do not require hover/tap selection. Owner car status remains green text, but without green outline; a dark neutral marker stroke is acceptable. <code>PadPathGuideClient</code> no longer uses <code>NextPadId</code> fallback: the authorized objective target route is hidden. When a target exists, the pointer must follow a segmented walkable route around collidable geometry, not a direct Beam through buildings.
- **Notifications/arrival.** The general <code>NotificationPresenter</code> deduplicates event key and owns the sound table <code>arrival/success/warning/blocked/track</code>. Legacy <code>PHONE OPENED/CLOSED</code>, <code>SELL PREVIOUS CAR FIRST</code> and duplicate <code>SALE SPOTS FULL</code> are removed. Immediate <code>SELLER ARRIVED / READY TO NEGOTIATE</code> is completely removed. After the seller physically exits, the server waits 30 seconds and only for the same manual <code>tradeId</code> in authoritative <code>Offered</code>, under <code>Arriving=false</code>, absence of prompt interaction, and disabled listing automation, publishes one deduplicated reminder <code>SELLER WAITING</code>. Any approach/interaction, deal change, automation, or seller state closure cancels the reminder.
- **Server flow/economy/first drive.** <code>RepairCompleted</code> idempotently places a Ready car in the listing queue; W/P reservation, objective stage, and timestamps are set only once. Reconcile releases a stale lease, returns Ready, and removes <code>listingBusy</code> after a failed staff coroutine. After driver exit, arrival/state/negotiation are published in a single transition without immediate screen notice. Experimental <code>BUSINESS +$X / 60S</code> has been removed: <code>EconomyRateService</code> is not initialized and does not receive station/dealer-sale records, HUD does not create <code>BusinessRate</code> and does not listen to <code>EconomyRateUpdate</code>; dormant remote/module is retained only as a compatibility surface. <code>trackIntroCompleted</code> is persisted; until the first finish, boarding in a personal car may show a side-objective <code>FIRST DRIVE • REACH MAIN TRACK</code>, and finish provides a short sound/local VFX and 2…4-second earnings result.
- **Personal Garage Customize.** Body Paint starts with the exact saved factory color, then shows remaining factory → owned universal → new universal. Headlight <code>RAINBOW</code> — the sixth square swatch and mutually-exclusive color mode: when active, static swatches are not selected, stored static fallback is canonically white, and selecting any regular color returns <code>steady</code>. Brightness is independent; separate mode controls go <code>COP / FLASHING / STEADY</code>. Phone uses 38×38 swatches/3 px gap, tablet 44×44, desktop 48×44.
- **FTUE listing + Customize preview.** The first tutorial car received by FirstSalesman gets a separate quick W→P profile (reverse-out 13, forward 30, reverse-park 12), without altering normal ambient automation. Unsaved Neon is shown only within <code>BODY → NEON</code>; outside this section, preview must reflect the saved state. Locked-copy preserves a black outline, highlights the target station in bright yellow, and <code>TUNING ATELIER</code> in bright cyan. Reveal <code>YOUR NEW / PERSONAL CAR</code> scales the target distance fully and does not overlap a distant vehicle on iPhone.
- **Owned-car previews / Settings.** Any card or showcase of a specific owned personal car displays its saved body paint, tint, stance, plates, headlights, and Neon through a single <code>PersonalVehiclePreview</code>; factory/catalog/dealer/customer images are not replaced by personal customization. Settings does not contain a service signature <code>CHANGES APPLY IMMEDIATELY</code>, and the compact version label at the bottom-right shows <code>V3.13</code>.
- **Tycoon release / service-proxy lifecycle.** Tycoon release is destructive: the server clears the incoming queue, <code>DealerCars</code>, Repair/Car Wash/Tuning service slots, vehicle/detached parking journeys, <code>BayStaff</code> and tow state. The client without a numeric <code>OwnerUserId</code> never holds tycoon visual interest by distance and destroys the run through <code>discardRun</code>, without using handoff-aware stash. Normal service car transfer between stations of an occupied tycoon remains unchanged.

- **Purchase-pad affordability/lifecycle.** Desktop and mobile use the same <code>BuildAnimationClient</code> renderer, but it does not perform proactive affordability styling: until owner/open pad, it maintains the normal authored appearance. Only an authoritative insufficient-funds response on physical touch of the pad arms the grey surface and changes the standard <code>Price</code> to exact <code>NEED $X MORE</code>; a compact notification is preserved. Cash ≥ cost, close/purchase, owner/id change, or removal fully restores authored material/color/font/text properties and canonical price. <code>PersonalGarageSecondBay</code> uses the same renderer/150×56 billboard/GothamBlack+FredokaOne with the same server constant $2,500,000 and must appear in the same claim/start lifecycle as other open owner pads. Its runtime-owned <code>GarageExpansionPad</code> is excluded from the general <code>PadService</code> owner-visibility cache: only <code>PersonalGarageService</code> manages its visibility/touch/query/open state, and a separate server-authoritative purchase flow is unchanged.

After the exclusion and infrastructure version 5, two fresh Play sessions (iPhone 13 844×390 and desktop 1446×793) produced <code>Transparency=0 / CanTouch=true / CanQuery=true / PurchasePadOpen=true / _O0=nil</code> from the first server sample throughout the 9.1/5.1-second window. On iPhone, 13, and on desktop, <code>Title/Price</code>, remained visible at 18 and 55 studs; the billboard retained normal <code>MaxDistance=70</code> and received neither <code>WorldLabelClass/WorldLabelTapOnly</code> nor a compact marker. Client/server: <code>MessageError=0</code>. Studio was returned to Edit and device simulation was stopped.

### ALREADY FIXED / REGRESSION VERIFIED

- The navigation arrow is not shown during automatic parking by the salesman; the existing path was not rewritten.
- The positive aspects of the report were retained: numeric pad effects, <code>Engine/Wheels/Body</code>, <code>SELL/ASK FOR MORE</code>, weather, and the transparent garage.

### DEFERRED

- Development of MainTrack alongside progression, road shortening/restructuring, a standalone mini-game or new revenue stream during waiting periods, overall economic rebalancing, and new content. Feedback "first five minutes slow; raise sale prices/accelerate second mechanic" is recorded as balance input, but not implemented in this iteration.
- Release acceptance on physical iPhone 13 and full manual 10-cycle soak <code>arrival → repair → Ready → salesman → display → buyer</code> remain release gates; simulator/source proof is not issued for physical device acceptance.
- Exact manual verification of failed-touch presentation on physical iPhone 13 remains part of release acceptance. Performance blocker for this scenario has already been closed: after removing the self-feeding Price observer, the owner confirmed normal Play, and MCP observed a responsive live Client; the previous restriction on re-Play until fix is no longer in effect.

### POST-PLAY CLIENT HANG — ROOT CAUSE FIXED / VERIFIED MCP PLAY 2026-09-01

- The initial root-cause session was read-only and did not initiate a new Play over a hung process. After fixing, the owner restarted Play and confirmed normal operation. In the currently selected Studio <code>Sell Cars V2</code> (<code>placeId 130009689985995</code>) MCP also observed a responsive live Client, ongoing gameplay/service transitions, and subsequent return to Edit without a reoccurrence of main-thread freeze.
- Roblox process logs refuted the previous statement of "hang on startup." Three relevant V2 sessions reached <code>[Tycoon] systems online</code>, <code>[BuildAnim] client online</code>, and <code>PlaySoloSuccess</code> within 2.97–4.66 s. After that, the Studio stopped responding.
- In three independent V2 logs, Roblox recorded <code>FLog::LuaHangedScript</code> for <code>Players.dnlvstudio.PlayerScripts.BuildAnimationClient</code>. The first stack points to <code>refreshPadAppearance</code> and <code>bindPadPriceText</code>; the next two reproduce the same current lines 698/704 and 632/704. After test termination <code>StudioHangMonitor</code> logs <code>MainThreadHangs</code>. OOM/fatal allocation in these sessions was not found.
- Messages <code>Connection fell back to legacy networking</code>, <code>Operation failed. Please check your network connection</code>, and <code>Error getting metadata for related place</code> exist, but are not the cause of this hang: the runtime already received <code>PlaySoloSuccess</code>, and the Roblox hang detector identified the executable game LocalScript and the exact stack.
- MCP audit of all <code>221</code> live <code>LuaSourceContainer</code> (<code>3,114,452</code> source chars) resulted in <code>0</code> compile failures. All identified <code>15 while true</code> and <code>3 repeat</code> loops either yield or have deterministic exits; no unconditional startup busy-loop was found.
- Root cause is located in the recently added mobile change set code <code>bindPadPriceText</code>. It binds each pad Price label on <code>GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text")</code>; a callback via <code>task.defer</code> re-invokes <code>refreshPadAppearance</code>, which through <code>renderPadPrice/writePrice</code> writes the same <code>Text</code>. The guard exists only around the immediate setter and does not protect against delayed/replicated signal delivery. Moreover, <code>PadService</code> server-side writes <code>NumberFormat.short(cost)</code>, while the client renderer restores <code>"$" .. NumberFormat.short(cost)</code>, meaning two writers use different canonical strings.
- Scale makes the error fatal: Edit snapshot contains <code>1,311</code> <code>BuyPad*</code> parts and exactly <code>1,311</code> Price labels. Therefore, initial hydration, attributes, server refresh, and client reconciliation create a massive queue of repeated refreshes, in which hang stacks and stall <code>refreshPadAppearance</code>. A global scan of <code>80,082</code> descendants takes only about 0.05 s, but it expands unsafe observer registration to pads outside normal zones.
- **FIX APPLIED / VERIFIED MCP PLAY:** <code>Price.Text</code> observer, <code>bindPadPriceText</code>, transient write guard, and global <code>Workspace.DescendantAdded</code> attributed-pad scan are removed from <code>BuildAnimationClient</code>. Price label now updates only based on server-authored pad attributes, owner/open/CanTouch, Cash for already armed pads, exact insufficient-funds receipt, and one-time <code>DescendantAdded</code> upon actual label appearance. The dynamic Second Garage Bay is lazily registered from exact authoritative receipt. <code>PadService</code> and client renderer use the same normal price format <code>$ + NumberFormat.short(cost)</code>. The current MCP source scan yielded <code>0</code> subscriptions <code>GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text")</code>, <code>0</code> global <code>workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect</code>, and <code>0</code> global <code>workspace:GetDescendants()</code>; only local zone/pad listeners and exact <code>InsufficientFunds</code> receipt are retained. <code>BuildAnimationClient</code>, <code>FundsFeedbackClient</code>, <code>PadService</code>, and <code>PersonalGarageService</code> have been re-tested in Edit-mode <code>loadstring</code>. Owner Play and bounded MCP live observation no longer reproduce freeze; a separate release acceptance of the failed-touch UX on a physical iPhone 13 remains mandatory.

---

## 26. Final reminder for another AI

Before making any change, ask yourself:

1. Am I modifying the current game or the target concept?
2. If this is UI, did I plan, implement, and verify it in the order iPhone/mobile → tablet → desktop, not the reverse?
3. Did I find an executable consumer, not just config/comment?
4. Does the server still decide money and state?
5. Does the change survive leave/rejoin and old profile?
6. Have I accounted for W1/W2, P1…P6 and all eight tycoon?
7. Have I avoided using an archive/template as a live dependency?
8. Have I checked the tutorial, automation, and manual path?
9. Have I updated this file if I changed the canonical contract?

If any answer is 'I don't know', first investigate, then modify.
